### [1] [CHOREO: Every Humanoid Skill as a Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22274)
*Ziyi Sun,Jingwen Chen,Yuxi Wang,Xiuze Xia,Long Cheng,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Yujun Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CHOREO提出一种免训练框架，将异构人形机器人技能统一为可执行运动轨迹SkillMotion，并通过直接衔接、Hermite混合或桥接动作进行组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人技能虽多，但因表示、接口与控制器不兼容而彼此孤立，难以组合复用。

Method: 核心观点是任何技能最终都可表示为可执行运动轨迹；据此将各技能转换为SkillMotion统一表示，融合运动状态、接触、语义与边界条件，并采用直接延续、三次Hermite混合或经验证的桥接运动进行组合，无需重训练源模型或测试时更新模型。

Result: 在MuJoCo中的Unitree G1上，CHOREO组织2,950个来自异构来源的SkillMotion资产，在130个多动作任务上达到95.4%序列成功率，八动作序列成功率为93.8%。

Conclusion: 可执行轨迹为累积与组合预训练人形机器人能力提供了可扩展接口。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics have produced diverse skills through reinforcement learning, motion imitation, and generative modeling. Yet these capabilities remain siloed because they are built around incompatible representations, interfaces, and controllers. We present CHOREO, a framework for training-free composition of heterogeneous humanoid skills. Our key observation is that, regardless of how a skill is learned, it can ultimately be expressed as an executable motion trajectory. Based on this observation, CHOREO converts each capability into SkillMotion, a unified representation that combines motion states, contacts, semantics, and boundary conditions. Skills are composed through direct continuation, cubic Hermite blending, or validated bridge motions, without retraining source models or updating models at test time. On Unitree G1 in MuJoCo, CHOREO organizes 2,950 admitted SkillMotion assets derived from heterogeneous sources and achieves 95.4\% sequence success across 130 multi-action tasks, including 93.8\% success on eight-action sequences. These results demonstrate that executable trajectories provide a scalable interface for accumulating and composing pretrained humanoid capabilities.

</details>


### [2] [D3DWA: Adaptive Weight and Prediction-Horizon for Dynamic Window Approach via Dueling Double Deep Q-Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22276)
*Zahra Jooyandeh,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出D3DWA：基于Dueling Double DQN的自适应DWA框架，在每个控制步同时选择DWA评价权重与预测时域，保留DWA的轨迹生成与碰撞检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DWA广泛用于局部导航，但性能高度依赖导航前固定的参数。尤其预测时域应随局部自由空间变化：开阔区域需较长时域以提升运动效率，狭窄或拥挤区域需较短时域以保证可行运动。

Method: 采用Dueling Double Deep Q-Network（D3QN），从连续导航状态中在每个控制步联合选择DWA的评价权重与预测时域，同时保持DWA原有的轨迹生成与碰撞检测机制不变。

Result: 在八个仿真环境（含未见过布局）中，D3DWA均到达目标。真实机器人实验中，D3DWA完成全部三种测试配置，其中包括仅调权重变体超时的受限场景。

Conclusion: 联合自适应评价权重与预测时域优于仅调整权重，验证了二者协同适应的必要性。

Abstract: The Dynamic Window Approach (DWA) is widely used for local navigation, but its performance depends strongly on parameters that are typically fixed before navigation. In particular, the appropriate prediction horizon can vary with local free space: longer horizons support efficient motion in open areas, whereas shorter horizons help preserve feasible motions in narrow or cluttered regions. This paper proposes D3DWA, an adaptive DWA framework based on a Dueling Double Deep Q-Network (D3QN), which jointly selects the DWA evaluation weights and prediction horizon from a continuous navigation state at every control step while retaining DWA's trajectory generation and collision checking. In eight simulated environments, including unseen layouts, D3DWA reached every goal. Real-robot experiments further showed that D3DWA completed all three tested configurations, including a constrained case in which the weights-only variant timed out. These results demonstrate the benefit of jointly adapting the evaluation weights and prediction horizon. Additional material is available at https://mertcookimg.github.io/d3dwa/

</details>


### [3] [DeViGrasp: Robust Visual Mobile Grasping for Quadruped Manipulators under Degraded Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22278)
*Liang Zhou,Jiaming Su,Yancong Wei,Kangkang Dong,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出退化的视觉移动抓取基准 DeViGrasp-Bench 与师生式全身控制框架 DeViGrasp-Net，在视觉退化、未见物体和复杂地形下优于 VBC 与 DQ-Net。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机械臂可实现复杂环境中的移动抓取，但其全身控制策略对机载视觉感知的不可靠性较为脆弱。现有方法多在相对可靠的观测条件下开发，尚未系统考察遮挡、分割掩码丢失、深度噪声与目标定位抖动对抓取推理和目标跟踪的影响。

Method: 1) 构建 DeViGrasp-Bench 基准：引入可控视觉退化、已见与未见物体、多难度等级和复杂地形，评测任务成功率、执行效率与动作平滑度。2) 提出 DeViGrasp-Net 师生框架：特权教师基于物体、机器人、末端执行器与任务状态，对离线抓取候选进行注意力建模；可部署学生融合双视角分割深度观测，并通过 Target Hold Memory 与 Temporal Memory Attention 整合当前、记忆与恢复目标假设。

Result: DeViGrasp-Net 在各退化等级、未见物体及复杂地形上均优于 VBC，并在所有评测退化等级上超过适配后的 DQ-Net。Difficult 设置下成功率达 62.3%，较 VBC 和 DQ-Net 分别提升 16.1 与 4.3 个百分点；Hard 设置下对 DQ-Net 的领先扩大到 10.5 个百分点。消融实验验证了抓取感知监督与可靠性感知时序记忆的互补作用。

Conclusion: 通过退化视觉基准与可靠性感知的师生式时空记忆框架，可显著提升四足机械臂在视觉退化、未见目标与复杂地形下的移动抓取鲁棒性与任务表现。

Abstract: Quadruped manipulators enable mobile grasping in complex environments, yet their whole-body control policies remain vulnerable to unreliable onboard visual perception. Existing methods are typically developed under relatively reliable observations and have not systematically examined how occlusion, segmentation-mask dropout, depth noise, and target-localization jitter affect grasp reasoning and target tracking. To address this gap, we introduce DeViGrasp-Bench, a benchmark for mobile grasping under degraded vision that incorporates controlled visual degradations, seen and unseen objects, multiple difficulty levels, and complex terrains, and evaluates task success, execution efficiency, and action smoothness. We further propose DeViGrasp-Net, a teacher--student framework that combines state-conditioned grasp reasoning with reliability-aware temporal target estimation. The privileged teacher attends to offline grasp candidates conditioned on object, robot, end-effector, and task states, while the deployable student fuses dual-view segmented-depth observations with current, memory, and recovery target hypotheses through Target Hold Memory and Temporal Memory Attention. DeViGrasp-Net outperforms VBC across degradation levels, unseen objects, and complex terrains, and surpasses an adapted DQ-Net across all evaluated degradation levels. Under the Difficult setting, it achieves a success rate of 62.3\%, improving upon VBC and DQ-Net by 16.1 and 4.3 percentage points, respectively; under the Hard setting, its margin over DQ-Net increases to 10.5 percentage points. Ablation studies confirm the complementary benefits of grasp-aware supervision and reliability-aware temporal memory.

</details>


### [4] [ORDER: A Fictitious-World Benchmark for Domain-Adaptive Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22285)
*Sai Krishna Reddy Sathi,Anuj Tiwari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ORDER 基准，基于虚构物理世界构建合成语料与任务，用于区分持续预训练中的"新学习"与"已有知识"，并将其贯穿到机器人感知—执行全流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练评估存在根本性混淆：若训练语料与模型已有知识重叠，性能提升无法归因于真正的新学习。该问题在知识密集、任务轻量（KHTL）的机器人部署（如药品分发、危险品处理、机构专有规程）中尤为关键——物理操作简单但规则专有且安全攸关，而大量实机测试昂贵甚至不安全。

Method: 构建 ORDER（Ontology-driven Decision-making for Embodied Reasoning）基准：一个 342,069 token 的虚构世界合成语料，定义自洽且不可能出现在任何模型预训练数据中的物理规律。基准包含 500 题的 ORDER-BENCH 知识测试，以及更难的组合任务 ORDER-SPATIAL——在熟悉与新场景中对物体排序以实现安全操作。随后将该基准贯穿到机器人流水线，加入技能适配阶段，并在带有人类在环纠正的仿真 iiwa7 机械臂上验证完整感知—执行环路。

Result: GPT-4.1 未适配时在 ORDER-SPATIAL 上低于随机水平（Kendall's tau = 0.441），说明其先验与虚构物理相冲突。持续预训练后，小模型在熟悉与全新场景上均显著提升，表明发生的是真正的世界模型归纳而非记忆。在机器人流水线中，知识测试得分高的模型往往无法产出有效可执行计划，需额外技能适配阶段；适配后，小型全离线模型在完整感知—执行环路中超过 GPT-4.1，即使后者可检索相同规则（Kendall's tau = 0.848 vs. 0.606）。

Conclusion: ORDER-SPATIAL 的表现（而非知识测试准确率）才是预测真实规划质量的关键指标；持续预训练带来的是可泛化的世界模型归纳能力，而机器人部署还需额外的技能适配阶段。

Abstract: Adapting language models to new domains via continual pre-training raises a basic evaluation problem: if the training corpus overlaps with what the model already knows, performance gains cannot be cleanly attributed to new learning rather than pre-existing knowledge. This matters most for knowledge-intensive, task-light (KHTL) robot deployments - pharmaceutical dispensing, hazardous-material handling, facility-specific protocols, where the physical task is simple but the governing rules are proprietary and safety-critical, and where extensive live testing is costly or unsafe. We introduce ORDER (Ontology-driven Decision-making for Embodied Reasoning), a benchmark built on a fictitious world: a 342,069-token synthetic corpus defining a self-consistent physics that cannot appear in any model's pre-training data. ORDER pairs a 500-question knowledge test (ORDER-BENCH) with a harder compositional task, ORDER-SPATIAL: ordering objects for safe manipulation across both familiar and entirely novel scenes. GPT-4.1 without adaptation scores below chance on ORDER-SPATIAL (Kendall's tau = 0.441), showing its priors actively conflict with the invented physics. After continual pre-training, small models improve substantially on both familiar and novel scenes alike evidence of genuine world-model induction rather than memorization. We then carry this through to a robot pipeline: models that answer the knowledge test well often cannot produce valid, executable plans without a further skill-adaptation stage, after which small, fully offline models outperform GPT-4.1 even when GPT-4.1 is given retrieval access to the same rules (Kendall's tau = 0.848 vs. 0.606), on a full perception-to-execution loop demonstrated on a simulated iiwa7 arm with human-in-the-loop correction. Throughout, ORDER-SPATIAL performance, not knowledge-test accuracy is what predicts real plan quality.

</details>


### [5] [OJOx: Specification-Conditioned Demonstrations for Embodied AI in Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22289)
*Mohamed Dawod*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"规范条件演示"(specification-conditioned demonstration)，并设计OJOx采集接口，在建筑场景中同步记录示范者感知的物理状态、外部设计给出的目标状态以及连接两者的行为，为具身智能提供可学习"按规范行动"的数据接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模自我中心与全身人体演示数据记录了人感知和做了什么，却很少记录赋予动作目的的外部规范。在建筑等场景中，技能工作指向设计模型中定义的项目特定配置，而这些配置在被观察的环境中尚不存在。工匠可迁移的能力不是某面墙的几何形状，而是从规范实现新几何的能力。

Method: 提出规范条件演示：对示范者感知的物理状态、外部设计提供的预期状态、以及连接两者的行为进行同步记录。实现OJOx采集接口：将设计几何传送到头显，锚定在物理工作空间，渲染到示范者的立体透视视图，并与全身及手部运动同步记录。开展一次完整装配的采集会话（33个组件的墙体，规范在工作过程中发生变化），并用独立配准的外部相机对照物理场景校验记录。

Result: 完成了33组件墙体的完整仪器化会话，记录经独立配准的外部相机校验与物理场景一致；记录的会话与现有人形机器人重定向基础设施兼容，并可在仿真中回放到Unitree G1机器人上。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个数据接口，用于检验具身策略能否不仅模仿已示范的动作，还能朝向训练经验中不存在的规范行动。

Abstract: Large-scale egocentric and whole-body human demonstrations are becoming a primary source of data for embodied intelligence. They record what people perceive and do, but rarely the external specification that gave an action its purpose. In construction that omission is consequential: skilled work is directed at project-specific configurations defined in a design model - configurations not yet present in the environment being observed. A mason's transferable competence is not the geometry of one wall but the ability to realise a new geometry from a specification. We introduce the specification-conditioned demonstration: a synchronised record of the physical state a demonstrator perceives, the intended state supplied to them by an external design, and the behaviour connecting the two. We present OJOx, a capture interface that realises this for construction - delivering design geometry to a headset, anchoring it in the physical workspace, rendering it into a demonstrator's stereo passthrough view, and recording that view synchronously with whole-body and hand motion. We report one fully instrumented session - a 33-component wall laid against a specification that changes while the work proceeds - and check the record against the physical scene through an external camera registered independently of the capture. Recorded sessions remain compatible with existing humanoid retargeting infrastructure and replay onto a Unitree G1 in simulation. The result is a data interface for testing whether embodied policies can learn not merely to imitate demonstrated actions, but to act toward specifications absent from their training experience.

</details>


### [6] [When Does Test-Time Physical Diagnosis Pay? A Frozen Policy Buys Evidence It Never Reads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22299)
*Zhengshu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出机器人物理诊断的六项有序经验条件链，并在受控与公开环境中测试其是否成立；发现迁移到未见机制后链条在"证据使用"环节断裂，且失败集中于最丰富证据层级的决策，被聚合准确率所掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人面对陌生物理条件时的常见做法是收集证据、推断变化并适应，但"诊断能改善行为"这一假设本身缺乏检验。作者希望提出一套可逐环检验的条件框架，判断诊断链条究竟在哪一环失效，而非仅凭恢复准确率或总体性能判断成败。

Method: 提出六项有序经验条件：(1) 有意义的参考基准；(2) 物理条件的可辨识性；(3) 所获证据被实际使用；(4) 决策价值；(5) 相对于固定替代方案的择优价值；(6) 安全实现。在受控环境与公开环境中逐一检验该链条，并通过冻结解码器的选择是否随完整轨迹改变、以及仅用轨迹增量拟合的线性模型在未参与拟合的机制上能否恢复正确选择，来判断证据是否被使用。

Result: 在受控环境中链条端到端成立；迁移到未见机制后链条在"证据使用"环节断裂——冻结解码器在需要完整轨迹的决策上不改变其选择，而仅用轨迹增量的线性模型可在被排除于拟合之外的机制上恢复正确选择，说明轨迹含信息却未被使用。失败集中于最丰富证据层级的决策，这些决策退化为随机水平，而依赖低成本证据即可确定的决策仍保持正确，二者差异被聚合准确率掩盖。在公开环境中，同一链条也可能在其他环节断裂。

Conclusion: 成功的物理辨识既不能保证证据被使用，也不能保证适应有效。评估应定位于诊断链条断裂的具体环节，而不应依赖恢复准确率或聚合性能指标。

Abstract: When a robot faces unfamiliar physical conditions, a common approach is to collect evidence about what changed and adapt. For such diagnosis to improve behavior, six ordered empirical conditions must hold: a meaningful reference, identifiability of the physical condition, use of the acquired evidence, decision value, selection value over a fixed alternative, and safe realization. We test this chain in controlled and public environments. It holds end to end in our controlled environments. After transfer to unseen mechanisms, however, it breaks at evidence use. On decisions requiring the full trace, the frozen decoder does not change its choice. A linear model using only trace increments recovers the correct choice on mechanisms excluded from fitting, showing that the trace is informative but unused. The failure is concentrated at the richest evidence level: those decisions fall to chance, while decisions settled with lower-cost evidence remain correct, a split hidden by aggregate accuracy. The same chain can fail at other links in public environments. Successful physical identification therefore guarantees neither evidence use nor useful adaptation; evaluation should identify where the chain breaks rather than rely on recovery accuracy or aggregate performance alone.

</details>


### [7] [EditWM: Event-Decomposed World Modeling with Incremental Correction for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22317)
*Junjie Yang,Qingwei Zeng,Youyou Li,Zicheng Ding,Ziyi Shi,Shuqi Shen,Hongliang Lu,Hai Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出EditWM，将自动驾驶世界模型分解为"正常演化"与"事件驱动的增量修正"，在紧凑视觉特征空间中进行未来预测，并将修正后的未来特征用于轨迹评分，在NAVSIM上取得更优的未来特征预测与竞争性的轨迹选择性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶动力学在可预测性上存在差异：多数场景为规律性演化，而部分场景由事件触发偏离。统一预测难以兼顾，因此需要区分常规演化与事件诱发的偏差，并对其进行选择性修正。

Method: EditWM将未来预测分解为正常演化与事件驱动的增量修正：(1) 轨迹条件化的正常预测器给出基础预测，随后冻结以用于修正学习；(2) 修正解码器将基础预测与观测历史、规划动作对比，产生有界特征更新，并由可学习门控调节其贡献；(3) 修正后的未来特征通过候选特异性交叉注意力为轨迹打分，将世界建模与规划选择相连。推理时仅用过去与当前观测、自车状态及候选轨迹。

Result: 在NAVSIM navtest全部12,146个场景上，专家轨迹条件下的评估显示未来特征MSE较Normal基线降低5.35%，83.54%的场景有改善；使用官方EPDMS评估器在100分制下达到91.05 EPDMS。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将修正后的未来表征整合进规划，可改善未来特征预测并实现具有竞争力的轨迹选择。

Abstract: World models support autonomous driving by predicting the scene evolution associated with candidate trajectories. Driving dynamics differ in predictability, motivating a distinction between regular evolution and event-induced deviations that call for selective correction. We propose EditWM, a world model that decomposes future prediction into normal evolution and event-driven incremental correction in compact visual feature space. A trajectory-conditioned normal predictor provides the base forecast and is then frozen for correction learning. A correction decoder compares this forecast with observation history and planned actions, producing a bounded feature update whose contribution is regulated by a learned gate. The corrected future features condition trajectory scoring through candidate-specific cross-attention, linking world modeling to plan selection. At inference, EditWM uses only past and current observations, ego state, and candidate trajectories. Across all 12,146 NAVSIM navtest scenes, expert-trajectory-conditioned evaluation shows a 5.35\% reduction in future-feature MSE over Normal, with improvements in 83.54\% of scenes. The system achieves 91.05 EPDMS on a 100-point scale using the official EPDMS evaluator. These results demonstrate improved future-feature prediction and competitive trajectory selection when corrected future representations are integrated into planning.

</details>


### [8] [Embedding Physics Priors in Robot Learning: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22319)
*Mattia Piccinini,Lucas Schulze,Alice Plebe,Matteo Saveriano,Thomas Beckers,Yuan Gao,Oleg Arenz,Baha Zarrouki,Dingrui Wang,Finn Rasmus Schäfer,Jan Peters,Johannes Betz,Gastone Pietro Rosati Papini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于"物理嵌入机器人学习"（physics-embedded robot learning）的综述，提出统一分类法并梳理方法、应用与开源生态，指出物理先验是互补而非替代数据驱动的关键归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动方法在计算机视觉与自然语言处理中成果显著，但机器人领域受限于数据稀缺、真实世界交互复杂以及可靠运行需求。物理先验可编码物理规律与约束，以机器人特有的归纳偏置弥补数据不足，有望提升泛化性、可解释性与样本效率。然而现有文献在术语、方法和应用领域上高度碎片化，难以评估该领域进展，因此需要系统性综述。

Method: 综述覆盖广泛的物理先验、机器人应用与机器学习模型（从单层感知机到生成式基础模型）。核心是提出统一分类法，按物理嵌入方式将现有方法分为三类：物理引导的输入、数据与表征；物理编码的模型架构；物理约束的训练损失函数。基于该分类法，综述机器人动力学学习、轨迹规划、预测、控制与估计方法，并整理相应开源软件生态。

Result: 构建了统一的物理嵌入分类框架，系统梳理了动力学学习、规划、预测、控制与估计等任务的方法脉络及配套开源工具，并识别出该领域的关键开放挑战与未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 物理先验是特别适用于机器人领域的归纳偏置，与数据驱动学习互补而非替代，有助于通向更具泛化性、数据高效且可信赖的机器人系统。

Abstract: The rapid progress of artificial intelligence is reshaping robotics and accelerating the adoption of learning-based approaches. While purely data-driven methods have achieved remarkable success in computer vision and natural language processing, robotics remains constrained by limited data, complex real-world interactions, and the need for reliable operation. These challenges have motivated the exploration of physics-embedded robot learning, which embeds physics priors into learning algorithms. By encoding the underlying physical laws and constraints, physics priors can complement limited data with robotics-specific inductive biases, potentially improving generalization, interpretability, and sample efficiency. However, the literature on physics-embedded robot learning remains fragmented across terminology, methodologies, and application domains, making it difficult to assess this growing body of work. This survey reviews physics-embedded robot learning across a broad range of physics priors, robotics applications, and machine learning models, from single-layer perceptrons to generative foundation models. We adopt a unified taxonomy that classifies existing approaches according to their physics embedding: physics-guided inputs, data, and representations; physics-encoded model architectures; and physics-informed training loss functions. Building on this taxonomy, we review methods for robot dynamics learning, trajectory planning, prediction, control, and estimation, together with the corresponding open-source software ecosystem. We identify key open challenges, and outline promising future research directions. Overall, we argue that physics priors provide a particularly relevant robotics-specific inductive bias, complementing rather than replacing data-driven learning, and paving the way toward more generalizable, data-efficient, and trustworthy robotic systems.

</details>


### [9] [ReliCAD: From Uncertain LLM Generation to Reliable Parametric CAD Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22325)
*Peng Zheng,Xintong Dong,Chuanyang Li,Jiaxin Jing,Chuqi Han,Hailong Shen,Yanzhi Song,Zhouwang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReliCAD框架，将LLM的不确定生成转化为可靠的参数化CAD建模，实现99.8%有效率与0.8753 IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的 probabilistic 生成与CAD建模的确定性需求存在根本矛盾，导致可靠性、设计意图保持与几何有效性受限。现有方法依赖大规模标注数据、缺乏对设计意图的显式建模，且未充分利用CAD内核的确定性能力。

Method: 通过显式设计意图建模，将用户需求转化为结构化设计规格，显式建模几何关系、拓扑依赖与特征构建顺序；生成约束感知的参数化指令，并通过Agent-ready API调用CAD内核执行几何构建与约束求解；记录运行时证据，采用验证-反馈机制评估生成模型与设计规格的一致性，实现错误定位与迭代修复。

Result: 在公开HistCAD生成数据集及自建多粒度CAD编辑数据集上显著优于基线方法，达到99.8%的有效率和0.8753的IoU。

Conclusion: ReliCAD为自然语言交互式CAD建模提供了可验证、可修复、可泛化的方法。

Abstract: Large language models have shown considerable potential for natural-language-driven parametric CAD modeling. However, a fundamental contradiction exists between their probabilistic generation and the deterministic requirements of CAD modeling, resulting in limitations in reliability, design-intent preservation, and geometric validity. Existing methods typically rely on large-scale annotated datasets, lack explicit modeling of design intent, and underutilize the deterministic capabilities of CAD kernels. To address these limitations, we propose ReliCAD, a unified framework that transforms uncertain LLM generation into reliable parametric CAD modeling. Through explicit design-intent modeling, ReliCAD converts user requirements into structured design specifications and explicitly models geometric relations, topological dependencies, and feature construction order. It then generates constraint-aware parametric instructions and invokes the CAD kernel through an Agent-ready API to perform geometric construction and constraint solving. ReliCAD further records runtime evidence and employs a verification-feedback mechanism to assess consistency between the generated model and the design specifications, enabling error localization and iterative repair. Experiments on the public HistCAD generation dataset and our multi-granularity CAD editing dataset demonstrate that ReliCAD significantly outperforms baseline methods, achieving 99.8\% validity rate and 0.8753 IoU. ReliCAD provides a verifiable, repairable, and generalizable approach to natural-language-interactive CAD modeling.

</details>


### [10] [AffordanceWAM: Affordance-Aware Joint World-Action Modeling for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22332)
*Jiadi You,Qize Yu,Yue Chen,Minghong Cai,Zhide Zhong,Yuran Wang,Bowen Ping,Jiaqi Liang,Zhenhao Shen,Haodong Yan,Yinchuan Li,Ruihai Wu,Xiaojuan Qi,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 AffordanceWAM，一种以 affordance 为共享交互接口的生成式 World Action Model，联合预测未来 RGB、标量 affordance、affordance 热图与连续机器人动作，实现无需人类动作标签的人到机器人迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作需要预测场景演化、识别可行交互位置并决定动作。带动作标签的机器人视频监督直接但成本高、多样性低；第一视角人类视频交互丰富，却缺少机器人动作且在具身与外观上存在差异，二者难以直接桥接。

Method: 以预训练视频扩散 Transformer 为骨干，采用分离参数化的 World Expert 与 Action Expert，通过 Masked Joint Self-Attention 耦合，在统一 flow-matching 目标下联合预测未来 RGB 观测、Scalar Affordance 场、Affordance Heatmap 和连续机器人动作。用 object-centric 时空 affordance 表示作为人类与机器人视频共享的交互目标；人类视频监督三个未来世界流，机器人轨迹额外提供动作监督。

Result: 在 RoboCasa、CALVIN ABC→D 及真实世界操作任务上，方法持续优于仅 RGB 与仅机器人数据的基线。在固定机器人监督条件下，RoboCasa 性能随带 affordance 标注的人类视频规模增加而单调提升。

Conclusion: affordance 可作为视觉-语言-动作学习与人到机器人迁移的有效接口，在缺乏人类动作标签与重定向的情况下实现可泛化的机器人操作。

Abstract: Generalizable robot manipulation requires predicting how a scene will evolve, identifying where interactions are feasible, and determining how to act. Action-labeled robot videos directly supervise control but are costly and limited in diversity, whereas egocentric human videos capture diverse interactions but lack robot actions and differ in embodiment and appearance. We introduce AffordanceWAM, an affordance-aware generative World Action Model that represents object-centric spatiotemporal affordance through Scalar Affordance and Affordance Heatmap, within the generated future World. This representation grounds visual prediction in task-relevant objects and interaction regions for action generation, and provides shared interaction targets across human and robot videos. Built on a pretrained video diffusion Transformer, AffordanceWAM uses separately parameterized World and Action Experts, coupled through Masked Joint Self-Attention, to jointly predict future RGB observations, Scalar Affordance fields, Affordance Heatmaps, and continuous robot actions under a unified flow-matching objective. Human videos supervise all three future-World streams, whereas robot trajectories additionally provide action supervision, enabling transfer without human action labels or retargeting. Experiments on RoboCasa, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation demonstrate consistent gains over RGB-only and robot-data-only baselines. Under fixed robot supervision, RoboCasa performance improves monotonically as affordance-annotated human video scales. These results support affordance as an effective interface for both vision-language-action learning and human-to-robot transfer.

</details>


### [11] [Active Spatial Inspection for Effective and Efficient Embodied Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22385)
*Wenbin Wang,Xiang Bai,Yizhao Wang,Hang Sun,Dong Ren,Jie Qin,Qingquan Li,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出空间显式引导的主动探索框架ACE，通过证据感知与曝光引导运动，缓解早停与过度探索的矛盾，实现更高的任务成功率和探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身探索框架通常以空间上粗糙且间接的暗示性线索和方向引导智能体行为，难以判断线索充分性及是否需要进一步勘察，导致过早终止或过度延续，最终降低任务成功率与效率。

Method: 从空间显式视角重新审视具身探索，提出空间勘察引导的主动探索（ACE）框架，将证据支撑的感知与曝光引导的运动耦合，建立空间分辨的决策范式。证据支撑感知通过细粒度定位与聚焦验证整合，将暗示性线索转化为精确的决定性视觉支撑；曝光引导运动通过前瞻性优先与回顾性抑制进行方向判断，选择有前景的方向以实现高效空间推进。

Result: 大量实验表明，ACE在导航任务成功率上比先前最优基线高18.0%，在问答探索效率上高10.3%，推进了有效且高效的具身探索。

Conclusion: ACE通过空间显式的决策范式缓解了防止过早终止与避免不必要延续之间的张力，在任务成功率和探索效率上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Achieving high task success and efficiency remains a central pursuit in embodied exploration. Existing frameworks typically guide agent behavior through a spatially coarse and indirect assessment of suggestive cues and directions, yet such designs may struggle to judge cue sufficiency and the need for further inspection, leading to early termination or excessive continuation and ultimately reducing task success and efficiency. This work rethinks embodied exploration from a spatially explicit standpoint and introduces the spatial-inspection-guided ACtive Exploration (ACE) framework coupling evidence-grounded perception with exposure-informed movement and establishing a spatially resolved decision paradigm. Evidence-grounded perception strengthens inferential validity by integrating granular localization with focused verification, turning suggestive cues into precise decisive visual support. Exposure-informed movement promotes directional judgment through prospective prioritization and retrospective suppression, selecting promising directions for efficient spatial progress. ACE alleviates the tension between preventing early termination and avoiding unnecessary continuation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACE achieves 18.0% higher navigation task success and 10.3% higher exploration efficiency for question answering than prior state-of-the-art baselines, advancing effective and efficient embodied exploration.

</details>


### [12] [Behavior Trees for Robotic Systems: An Empirical Study on Practices and Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22404)
*Razan Ghzouli,Jennifer Horkoff,Daniel Strüber,Rebekka Wohlrab*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过混合方法研究（汽车公司技术行动研究+34名机器人从业者调查）揭示行为树（BT）在真实场景中的采用现状：BT提升了团队沟通与机器人决策逻辑可理解性，但从业者面临架构/语言选择、ROS集成、粒度决策、库与文档不足、节点排序及可扩展性等多重挑战，阻碍了更广泛采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年行为树（BT）已成为协调机器人系统任务的主流行为模型之一，但关于其在真实场景中采用的实证证据仍然有限，尤其缺乏来自学术界与工业界从业者经验与实践的证据。缺乏这种以从业者为基础的证据，研究社区可能制定出原理上合理却与实际挑战部分脱节的指南与工具，导致临时性做法泛滥，进而阻碍软件复用、维护与演化。

Method: 采用混合方法研究（mixed-methods study），结合在某汽车公司开展的技术行动研究（technical action research）与一项针对34名机器人从业者的调查。

Result: 1）BT改善了团队沟通并提升了机器人决策逻辑的可理解性，体现其超越任务协调的实用价值；2）从业者面临大量非平凡的设计与集成决策，因缺乏充分的指南与工具支持而更加复杂，涵盖实现BT及将其集成到ROS中的架构与语言选择，论文报告了观察到的模式；3）从业者面临多因素粒度决策，并对现有库的体验参差不齐；4）对于最常用的BT库，报告了实现挑战与文档缺口；5）在规划算法方面，从业者认为确定最优BT节点顺序存在困难；6）关于可扩展性的看法尚无定论，当前库与实践的局限阻碍了更广泛的采用。

Conclusion: 论文总结了跨领域的观察结论，并针对在机器人系统中采用BT的从业者，以及旨在推进BT采用实证理解的研究者，提出了相应启示。

Abstract: Over the last decade, behavior trees (BT) have become one of the dominant behavior models for coordinating missions of robotic systems. Yet empirical evidence on BT adoption in real-world contexts remains limited, especially regarding practitioners experiences and practices. Without practitioner-grounded evidence from both academic and industrial settings, the research community risks developing guidelines and tools that are plausible in principle but only partly aligned with real challenges. This scarcity of evidence leads to ad-hoc practices, impeding software reuse, maintenance, and evolution. This paper reports a mixed-methods study combining a technical action research investigation at an automotive company with a survey of 34 robotics practitioners. Our results indicate that BTs improve team communication and the understandability of robotic decision-making logic, reflecting BTs practical value beyond mission coordination. At the same time, practitioners face multiple non-trivial design and integration decisions, complicated by the lack of adequate guidelines and tool support. These decisions span architectural and language choices when implementing BTs and integrating them within ROS, for which we report observed patterns. Additionally, practitioners faced multi-factor granularity decisions and mixed experiences with current libraries. For the most used BT libraries, they reported implementation challenges and documentation gaps. Regarding planning algorithms, practitioners find deciding optimal BT node ordering challenging. Views on scalability remained inconclusive, and limitations in current libraries and practices hinder broader adoption. We conclude with cross-cutting observations and implications for both practitioners adopting BTs in robotic systems and researchers aiming to advance empirical understanding of BT adoption.

</details>


### [13] [VLPSA: Vision-Language-Poisson-Safe Actions for Full-Body Safety of Learned Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22462)
*Meg Wilkinson,Emily Fourney,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLPSA：一种无需重训练、基于在线泊松安全函数与CBF-QP滤波的全身安全过滤框架，为VLA策略在杂乱动态环境中提供碰撞避免保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型使机器人操作日益通用，但学得的策略不提供碰撞避免的安全保证，尤其在训练分布之外的环境中。现有方法需要重训练或缺乏全身安全性。

Method: 从感知数据在线合成泊松安全函数（PSF），得到控制障碍函数（CBF），通过CBF-QP安全滤波器对全身及被抓取物体（视为末端连杆延伸）施加约束；利用布尔CBF组合融合PSF的双分辨率，以在关键任务区域保持精细空间分辨率的同时实现实时部署。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO上与安全过滤基线对比，取得最高碰撞避免率：将基础π₀.₅策略的避碰率从23.1%提升至91.2%，并超过其任务成功率。在Franka FR3的杂乱场景、动态障碍与人为干扰下实现实时全身安全操作。

Conclusion: VLPSA无需重训练即可为VLA策略提供实时全身碰撞避免保证，在仿真与真机中均验证有效，兼顾安全性与任务性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable increasingly general-purpose robotic manipulation, but such learned policies do not provide safety guarantees for collision avoidance---especially in environments outside of training distributions. This work presents Vision-Language-Poisson-Safe Actions (VLPSA), a safety filtering framework that provides full-body safety for VLA policies in cluttered and dynamic environments without retraining. VLPSA synthesizes Poisson Safety Functions (PSF) online from perception data, yielding a Control Barrier Function (CBF) that is enforced through a CBF-QP safety filter over the full body and any grasped object, treated as an extension of the final robot link. To enable real-time deployment while maintaining fine spatial resolution in critical task regions, VLPSA combines dual resolutions of this PSF using Boolean CBF compositions. We evaluate VLPSA on SafeLIBERO against safety-filtering baselines, where it achieves the highest collision avoidance rate among the evaluated methods, increasing collision avoidance from 23.1% for the base $π_{0.5}$ policy to 91.2% while surpassing its task success rate. We further deploy VLPSA on a Franka FR3 in cluttered scenes with dynamic obstacles and human interference, demonstrating real-time full-body safety during manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [14] [Tracker-Free Robotic Ultrasound Calibration with a Spherical-Marker Phantom and Threshold-Free Center Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22483)
*Kyoungmo Koo,Guangshen Ma,Xueding Wang,Mark Draelos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需外部跟踪器的机器人超声标定框架，使用球形标记体模与无阈值感知管线定位球心，仅需单标记即可实现与三标记相当的高精度标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人超声标定需准确建立超声图像与机器人坐标系的映射，但现有方法通常依赖复杂体模或外部三维跟踪设备，难以实现准确且自动化的标定。

Method: 采用球形标记体模与高度自动化的感知管线进行球心定位；提出无阈值图像处理方法，依据每幅超声图像的强度分布定位球形特征，免去手工调参的强度阈值；利用球标记的多姿态观测，在无外部跟踪、无需预知球心在机器人基座坐标系位置的情况下估计超声到末端执行器（US-to-EE）的变换。

Result: 在机器人超声平台上通过重复球扫描验证，并用已知CAD模型的几何异构体模评估。12折交叉验证中，单标记标定的球心精度与精密度为1.75±0.51 mm与0.85±0.11 mm，三标记为1.72±0.51 mm与0.84±0.12 mm。三组重建中，单标记标定的配准后点到面MAE±SD为圆锥0.41±0.35 mm、三棱柱0.50±0.41 mm，与三标记的0.40±0.35 mm和0.48±0.40 mm接近。

Conclusion: 所提无跟踪器、单标记机器人超声标定框架可自动、鲁棒地完成标定，精度与三标记方法相当，且无需外部跟踪与手工阈值调参，具备良好实用性与跨成像设置的泛化能力。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound (US) calibration is essential for accurately relating US images to the robot coordinate system, but accurate and automated calibration remains challenging because existing methods often require complex phantoms or external 3D trackers. In this work, we develop a tracker-free robotic US calibration framework using a spherical-marker phantom and a highly automated perception pipeline for sphere-center localization. The proposed threshold-free image-processing method localizes the spherical feature based on each US image's intensity distribution, eliminating hand-tuned intensity thresholds and improving robustness across imaging settings without per-system retuning. Multi-pose observations of the spherical fiducial are then used to estimate the US-to-EE transformation without external tracking or prior localization of the sphere center in the robot base frame. We validate the proposed framework on a robotic US platform through repeated sphere scans and further assess the calibrated system using geometrically distinct phantoms with known CAD models. Across 12 cross-validation folds, single-marker calibration achieved sphere-center accuracy and precision of $1.75\pm0.51$ mm and $0.85\pm0.11$ mm, respectively, compared with $1.72\pm0.51$ mm and $0.84\pm0.12$ mm for three-marker calibration. Across the three reconstruction sets, the single-marker calibration yielded pooled post-registration point-to-surface MAE$\pm$SD values of $0.41\pm0.35$ mm for the cone and $0.50\pm0.41$ mm for the triangular prism, closely matching the three-marker results of $0.40\pm0.35$ mm and $0.48\pm0.40$ mm, respectively.

</details>


### [15] [Layered e-skin for Shear Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22493)
*Qingzheng Cong,Alexis W. M. Devillard,Abu Bakar Dawood,Xinxin Zhang,Wen Fan,Neri Niccolò Dei,Cem Suulker,Kaspar Althoefer,Etienne Burdet,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双层堆叠FSR阵列指尖传感器，通过层间CoP位移将剪切力转化为可测量信号，结合物理力矩平衡与CNN-GRU模型实现三轴力估计、接触运动跟踪与滑移检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FSR阵列仅能感知法向力，难以在机器人指尖实现剪切力估计与滑移检测，需要一种兼具高分辨率压力映射与剪切感知能力的结构方案。

Method: 设计双层FSR阵列，中间加入柔性点阵弹性体间隔层，将剪切载荷转化为层间位移，从而在两层之间产生相对压力中心（CoP）偏移；用基于物理的力矩平衡将层间CoP位移与剪切力关联，并采用端到端CNN--GRU模型（以两层信号为输入）建模负载相关压缩与接触重分布引起的非线性效应。

Result: 剪切力估计精度为F_x的R²=0.914、F_y的R²=0.944，持续优于单层基线。机械臂操作实验中，深层跟踪机器人手臂施加的平移与旋转，表层跟踪接触面滑移；两层总压力响应之差的瞬态变化在所有测试线索中取得最佳滑移事件检测性能。

Conclusion: 双层FSR阵列可超越传统法向力感知，同时提供三轴力估计、接触运动跟踪与滑移事件检测能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a stacked two-layer force-sensing resistor (FSR) array designed for robotic fingertips that combines high-resolution pressure mapping with shear-force estimation. A compliant lattice elastomer spacer converts shear loading into a measurable inter-layer displacement, producing relative center-of-pressure (CoP) shifts between layers. A physics-based moment balance links inter-layer CoP displacement to shear force, while an end-to-end CNN--GRU model captures nonlinear effects from load-dependent compression and contact redistribution. This model, with both layers as input, achieves coefficients of determination $R^2 = 0.914$ for $F_x$ and $R^2 = 0.944$ for $F_y$, consistently outperforming single-layer baselines for shear-force estimation. Robotic manipulation experiments show that, for contact-motion tracking, the deep layer tracks the translation and rotation imposed by the robot arm, whereas the superficial layer tracks the slip at the contact surface. Transient changes in the difference between the total pressure responses of the two layers provide the best slip-event detection performance among the tested cues. These results demonstrate that two-layer FSR arrays can provide three-axis force estimation, contact-motion tracking, and slip-event detection beyond conventional normal-force sensing.

</details>


### [16] [Latent Policy Steering: An Efficient and Flexible Framework for Cross-Embodiment Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22521)
*Yiqi Wang,Mrinal Verghese,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LPS通过跨具身预训练世界模型并在潜空间中搜索接近目标具身数据的动作计划，以少量目标演示显著提升多种策略在新具身上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人视觉运动策略高度依赖训练数据的规模与质量，但真实世界高质量演示采集成本高昂。大规模机器人/人类数据集虽日益丰富，却因具身差异与动作空间不匹配而难以直接利用。因此，跨具身迁移（复用其他具身经验以提升目标具身学习）成为将机器人学习扩展到单机器人数据采集之外的关键。

Method: 提出潜策略引导（Latent Policy Steering, LPS）框架。其包含：1）具身无关预训练阶段，基于光流在多样具身上训练图像世界模型（WM）；2）在目标具身上用机器人动作微调该WM；3）在WM潜空间中搜索贴近微调数据的动作计划，从而将基础策略引导向更优动作。LPS与策略无关，可灵活适配不同策略而无需重新训练。

Result: 在Robomimic与真实世界评测中，仅使用目标具身上的50条演示：（1）Diffusion Policy平均性能相对提升16%（仿真）与62%（真实）；（2）Pi0.5平均性能相对提升8%（仿真）与14%（真实）。

Conclusion: 通过学习跨具身共享的视觉动力学并有效利用稀缺的目标具身数据，LPS实现了高效跨具身迁移，且作为策略无关框架能即插即用地提升多种现有策略，为超越单机器人数据采集的规模化机器人学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The performance of learned robot visuomotor policies depends heavily on the size and quality of their training data, yet collecting high-quality demonstrations remains costly for robots in the real world. Although large-scale robot and human datasets are increasingly available, embodiment gaps and mismatched action spaces make them difficult to leverage directly. Cross-embodiment transfer, reusing experience from other embodiments to improve learning on a target embodiment, is therefore crucial for scaling robot learning beyond per-robot data collection.
  In this work, we find that efficient transfer can be achieved by learning from what is shared across embodiments, the visual dynamics of how the world responds to motion, and by effectively exploiting the scarce target-embodiment data at test time. The proposed framework, called Latent Policy Steering (LPS), implements an embodiment-agnostic pretraining phase, which trains an image-based World Model (WM) with optical flow across diverse embodiments. The resulting WM is finetuned on the target embodiment with robot actions. It then steers the base policy toward better actions by searching in the WM's latent space for plans that stay close to the finetuning data. LPS is a policy-agnostic framework: it can flexibly accommodate different policies without having to retrain them. In Robomimic and real-world evaluations, LPS improves the average performance of Diffusion Policy relatively by 16% and 62%, and Pi0.5 by 8% and 14%, with only 50 demonstrations on an unseen target embodiment.

</details>


### [17] [FRAMES: Failure Recovery And Monitoring of Embodied Skills for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22538)
*Ajay Vikram Periasami,Xinyuan Luo,Haoyu Li,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRAMES是一个面向Unitree G1人形机器人的故障感知监督框架，位于CEER全身控制器之上，由规划、监控、恢复三智能体与记忆模块组成；其中监控模块在MuJoCo中100次试验上取得94.0%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM规划器能分解指令并选择可复用技能，但选对技能并不保证物理执行成功；在人形loco-manipulation中，接近、抓取、搬运或放置阶段的错误会使长时程规划的后续部分失效，因此需要执行层面的故障感知与监督。

Method: 提出FRAMES监督框架：Planner Agent通过参数化中层技能选择子任务；基于VLM的Monitor Agent利用时序多视角观测和结构化机器人/接触证据评估每个技能；检测到故障后终止当前技能并向Recovery Agent提供有依据的反馈；框架还包含复用历史技能经验的Memory Module，以及基于深度与分割的几何grounding。

Result: 在MuJoCo中对监控模块独立评估：100次试验（5类任务，50次失败、50次成功）中，检出50次失败中的48次，正确接受50次成功中的46次，总体准确率94.0%。

Conclusion: 结果初步验证了监控组件的有效性；完整恢复闭环的端到端评估仍在进行中。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) planners can decompose natural-language instructions and select reusable robot skills, but choosing the correct skill does not guarantee successful physical execution. This gap is especially important in humanoid loco-manipulation, where errors during approach, grasping, transport, or placement can invalidate the remainder of a long-horizon plan. We present FRAMES, a failure-aware supervisory framework for the Unitree G1 humanoid that operates above the CEER whole-body controller. A Planner Agent selects subtasks through parameterized mid-level skills, while a vision-language-model-based Monitor Agent evaluates each skill using temporal multi-view observations and structured robot and contact evidence. Detected failures stop the active skill and provide grounded feedback to a Recovery Agent. The framework further includes a Memory Module for reusing prior skill experience, and geometric grounding via depth and segmentation. We independently evaluate the monitoring module of the framework in MuJoCo using 100 trials comprising 50 failed and 50 successful executions across five tasks. The monitor detects 48 of 50 failures, correctly accepts 46 of 50 successful executions, and achieves 94.0% overall accuracy. These results provide initial evidence for the monitoring component, while end-to-end evaluation of the complete recovery loop remains ongoing.

</details>


### [18] [React When You Need To: Event-Triggered Asynchronous Inference for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22587)
*Yansong Wu,Huaqing Li,Tianding Hou,Lingyun Chen,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出事件引导的动态推理策略，根据场景变化自适应调整推理间隔，兼顾运动一致性与反应实时性，在真实静态与动态环境中平均成功率达95%，超出最强基线55个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通常预测动作块，导致执行过程中难以对环境变化做出反应；现有异步推理方法虽提升了反应性，但依赖固定的推理间隔，无法根据实际场景变化灵活调整。

Method: 提出事件引导的动态推理策略：依据自上次推理以来观测到的场景变化，自适应地调整推理间隔（inference gap）。

Result: 在静态与动态真实世界环境中均表现最佳，平均成功率为95%，比最强基线高出55个百分点。

Conclusion: 事件引导的动态推理可同时保持运动一致性与及时反应能力，在动态场景中显著优于固定间隔方法。代码将在录用后公开。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly predict action chunks, limiting their ability to react to environmental changes during execution. Existing asynchronous inference methods improve reactivity but typically rely on a fixed inference gap. In this paper, we propose an event-guided dynamic inference strategy that adapts the inference gap according to scene changes observed since the previous inference. Thereby, it simultaneously preserves motion consistency and prompt reactivity. Across static and dynamic real-world settings, our method consistently performs best, averaging 95% success and exceeding the strongest baseline by 55 percentage points. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance. The project page is available at https://react-when-you-need-to.github.io/.

</details>


### [19] [REBOOT: From Failure to Recovery - A Dataset and Benchmark for Precision Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22591)
*Nana Yaw Owusu Ofori-Ampofo,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Joseph Thekinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出REBOOT——首个以失败为一等信号的机器人操作基准，包含18个精密装配任务、2160条演示，按五个共享阶段分解，并提供带专家恢复轨迹的失败样本，实现阶段级评估以揭示二元成功率掩盖的模型特异性失败点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习策略存在典型失败模式：在不确定状态停滞、接触密集对齐中漂移、精密任务中以毫米级偏差错过目标。然而训练数据多为成功演示，真实基准又常将性能简化为二元成功，既缺乏恢复监督，也无法分析失败发生的位置。

Method: 构建REBOOT基准：涵盖18个精密装配任务、2160条演示；每任务分解为Align(pick)、Engage(pick)、Transport、Align(place)、Engage(place)五个共享阶段。跨阶段引入失败并配以专家恢复轨迹，使系统回到有效延续状态。任务标注旋转对称性、啮合间隙精度层级，并通过匹配的安装-移除对标注装配方向；失败片段按阶段与类别化失败模式标注，可归因至运动学阶段与公差违规。数据包含四视角同步RGB-D观测及阶段级成功/失败条件的自然语言描述；一半为专家演示，另一半为反映模仿学习策略部署失败的恢复演示。对action-chunked transformer、diffusion、π0-FAST策略以阶段级完成率进行基准测试。

Result: 采用阶段级完成率评估action-chunked transformer、diffusion与π0-FAST策略，揭示出被二元评估掩盖的模型特异性失败点。数据集与代码见https://nanayawoa.github.io/REBOOT。

Conclusion: REBOOT将失败作为一等信号，通过阶段分解、失败标注与专家恢复轨迹，为机器人操作提供超越终端成功率的诊断性评估，能够定位策略在运动学阶段与公差层面的具体失败。

Abstract: Robot learning policies fail in characteristic ways: they stall in uncertain states, drift during contact-rich alignment, and miss targets by millimetres in precision tasks. Yet training datasets consist largely of successful demonstrations, while real-world benchmarks often reduce performance to binary success. This limits both supervision for recovery and analysis of where failures occur. We introduce REBOOT (Recovery Episode Benchmark for Off-nominal Trajectories), the first robot manipulation benchmark designed around failure as a first-class signal. REBOOT contains 2,160 demonstrations across 18 precision assembly tasks, each decomposed into five shared phases: Align(pick), Engage(pick), Transport, Align(place), and Engage(place), enabling phase-level evaluation beyond terminal success. Failures are introduced across phases and paired with expert recovery trajectories that return the system to a valid continuation state. Tasks are annotated with rotational symmetry, engagement-clearance precision tier, and assembly direction through matched install-remove pairs. Failure episodes are labeled by phase and categorical failure mode, enabling attribution to kinematic stage and tolerance violation. Data includes synchronized RGB-D observations from four viewpoints and grounded natural-language descriptions of phase-level success and failure conditions. Half the dataset contains expert demonstrations; the other half contains recovery demonstrations sampled to reflect failures observed in imitation-learned policy rollouts. We benchmark action-chunked transformer, diffusion, and $π_0$-FAST policies using phase-level completion rates, revealing model-specific failure points hidden by binary evaluation. Dataset and code: https://nanayawoa.github.io/REBOOT

</details>


### [20] [Characterizing Wildlife Response to Biomimetic and Conventional Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22594)
*Huy Pham,Levi Cai,Yogesh Girdhar,Daniela Rus,Zach J. Patterson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了在加勒比珊瑚礁现场对比传统螺旋桨驱动AUV、仿生海龟鳍驱动AUV与潜水员对鱼类扰动影响的数据集，并用计算机视觉量化鱼类行为变化，为仿生水下机器人生态监测影响提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV可规模化采集海洋生态数据，但可能惊扰本地鱼类，改变其行为，从而削弱其作为科学工具的价值。作者假设赋予AUV非捕食性动物的形态与行为（仿生）可减少鱼类的负面反应，因此需要实证数据比较不同监测方式造成的扰动。

Method: 在加勒比珊瑚礁生物多样性热点开展实验，设置三类transect条件：传统推进器驱动AUV、受海龟启发的鳍驱动仿生AUV以及潜水员。采集水下场景图像，利用计算机视觉对鱼类进行定位并分析其行为变化，进而比较不同条件下的扰动程度。

Result: 两种AUV均引起可测量的鱼类行为变化；经多重比较校正后，两机器人之间差异不再显著，但点估计总体上更有利于仿生AUV。潜水员transect期间鱼类反应大于AUV transect，但该探索性比较样本量小且存在若干混杂因素。作者强调受实验与操作限制，结论具有初步性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了首个已知数据集与基准，用于量化仿生机器人在野外生态监测中的行为影响。结果表明仿生设计可能有潜在优势，但效应有限且未达统计显著，需更大规模研究以解析微小效应并指导进一步优化设计；潜水员比较结果仅供初步参考。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are a promising alternative to divers for scalable collection of natural ocean ecology data. However, these robots may disturb local fauna and cause drastic behavioral differences compared to other monitoring techniques, decreasing their value as scientific tools. A promising prospect is to make AUVs that are more biomimetic, with the hope that taking on the form and behavior of a non-predatory animal may reduce adverse responses. We present the first dataset comparing fish disturbance in response to a conventional thruster-driven AUV, a sea turtle inspired flipper-driven AUV, and a diver. Experiments were conducted at a biodiversity hotspot in a Caribbean reef, and images of the scene were analyzed using computer vision to localize and study fish behavior change. Both AUVs cause measurable changes in fish behavior. Although no between-robot differences remain significant after correction for multiple comparisons, point estimates generally favor the biomimetic AUV, motivating larger studies capable of resolving modest effects and further design changes to optimize for disturbance. We also observe larger responses during diver transects than during AUV transects, although this exploratory comparison is based on a small diver sample with several confounds. While conclusions must be taken as preliminary due to operational and experimental limitations, this study provides the community with a first known dataset and benchmarks to quantify the behavioral impacts of biomimetic robots for ecological monitoring in the wild.

</details>


### [21] [VT-Bridge: Bridging Pretrained Foundation VLAs to VTLAs via Lightweight Residual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22606)
*Yansong Wu,Tuo Yang,Rongping Zhao,Lingyun Chen,Xiao Chen,Junnan Li,Fan Wu,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-Bridge：一种轻量残差适配策略，将预训练VLA基础模型桥接为VTLA模型，仅需少量视触觉示教数据和约0.98M参数适配器即可显著提升接触丰富操作任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VTLA模型在接触丰富操作中优于VLA，但其开发受限于海量视触觉数据与巨大计算资源需求。作者希望降低数据与训练门槛，避免从头训练或改动预训练VLA架构。

Method: 在预训练VLA骨干上采用统一下游轻量残差适配器架构（backbone-specific weights），在机器人执行频率上对动作进行残差修正；每任务仅需最多50条视触觉示教数据微调VLA骨干并训练0.98M参数的残差适配器。

Result: 在三种代表性VLA骨干（π0、π0.5、SmolVLA）和四个接触丰富操作任务上验证，平均任务完成率由仅任务级VLA微调的11.7%提升至62.9%，跨架构一致有效。

Conclusion: VT-Bridge在接触丰富操作中展现出广泛适用性、有效性与易用性，为高效构建VTLA模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-Tactile-Language-Action (VTLA) models have demonstrated clear advantages over Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in contact-rich manipulation. However, developing VTLA models is severely constrained by the massive amounts of vision-tactile data and computational resources required. To address this bottleneck, we propose VT-Bridge, a lightweight residual adaptation strategy that bridges pretrained foundation VLAs to VTLAs. Rather than training a VTLA model from scratch or modifying the original architecture of a pretrained VLA, VT-Bridge employs an identical lightweight residual-adapter architecture across VLA backbones and uses backbone-specific weights to refine actions at the robot execution frequency. This design substantially lowers the data and training barriers. Specifically, it requires up to 50 vision-tactile demonstrations per task to fine-tune a VLA backbone and train a 0.98M-parameter residual adapter. Experiments with three representative VLA backbones ($π_0$, $π_{0.5}$, and SmolVLA) across four contact-rich manipulation tasks further demonstrate its consistent effectiveness across VLA architectures. On average, VT-Bridge raises the task completion rate from 11.7% with task-level VLA fine-tuning alone to 62.9%. Together, these findings demonstrate the broad applicability, effectiveness, and accessibility of VT-Bridge for contact-rich manipulation. The project page is available at https://hoxnocha.github.io/vt-bridge-web/.

</details>


### [22] [Becoming a Fruit Ninja: Real-Time Probabilistic Kinodynamic Planning for Manipulator Projectile Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22608)
*Lucas Chen,Austin Garrett,Andrew Niu,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FRUITNINJA，一种在GPU上批量生长搜索树的anytime采样规划器，用于机器人投射物拦截；在仿真中切割成功率达96.7%（无障碍）与68.3%（五障碍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投掷物拦截是困难的动态操作问题：需在物体经过路径上某点时使机械臂到达该状态，同时切片还固定了接触时的刀刃速度与朝向，且必须在毫秒级、执行器限制内完成，因此目标只是机器人状态与到达时间的一个子集（拦截流形），规划难度大。

Method: FRUITNINJA是anytime采样规划器，在GPU上分批向拦截流形生长搜索树；每条边为精确三次曲线，其行程时间通过针对机械臂动力学的并行搜索求得，从而保证每条边满足执行器限制；规划按对物体位置不确定性与机械臂到达时间不确定性的风险感知目标进行排序。

Result: 在Franka Research 3上与六个基线在标定过的实时仿真中对比：FRUITNINJA在无障碍场景切割96.7%的投掷物，五障碍场景为68.3%；最优基线分别为68.3%和35.0%。

Conclusion: FRUITNINJA通过GPU批量并行生长拦截流形树、动力学验证的精确三次边以及风险感知排序，在实时投射物拦截（含切片）任务上显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Projectile interception is a challenging dynamic manipulation problem. Intercepting a thrown object with a robot arm requires reaching a point on the object's path as the object passes through it. Slicing also fixes the blade's velocity and orientation at contact. The goal is therefore a subset of the states of the robot and arrival times that moves as the object falls, and the arm must reach it within its actuator limits in milliseconds. We present FRUITNINJA, an anytime sampling-based planner that grows a tree on the GPU in batches toward the interception manifold. Each edge is an exact cubic whose travel time is found by a parallel search against the arm's dynamics, so every edge satisfies the actuator limits. Plans are ranked by a risk-aware objective over the uncertainty in the object's position and the arm's arrival time. We evaluate on a Franka Research 3 against six baselines in a calibrated real-time simulator, where FRUITNINJA cuts 96.7% of tosses in the open and 68.3% among five obstacles, versus the best baseline's 68.3% and 35.0% respectively.

</details>


### [23] [From Documented Strengths to Force Limits: Material-Informed Robotic Insertion for Construction Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22609)
*Lin He,Yanyi Chen,Haofei Sun,Lingyao Li,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE系统将文档材料证据转化为机器人插入装配的承载能力估计与可追溯决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人插入装配中，材料属性和装配条件差异使接触力选择困难；施工文档虽含工程知识，但难以转为特定装配的载荷限值。

Method: SAGE限定LLM仅从检索的段落和表格中提取拉伸与压缩强度并记录来源，再由响应模型插值离线有限元解，将强度与装配条件转换为轴向承载能力；正间隙配合用其设定轴向力上限，过盈配合则与实测支撑需求比较以决定是否准入。

Result: 主基准上平均容量误差由直接LLM估计的80.65%降至10.74%，未重新拟合时在16个新几何上平均误差为8.00%；在指定支撑释放模型下，62次仿真中正确分类59次且仅出现保守误差；xArm6实测中13次插入试验成功9次。

Conclusion: 将文档理解交给LLM、将力计算交给显式力学模型，可获得准确承载估计与可追溯的插入决策。

Abstract: Insertion is a fundamental operation in robotic construction assembly, where variations in material properties and assembly conditions make it difficult to select contact forces that complete the task without exceeding the assembly's capacity. Although construction documents encode engineering knowledge about materials and their conditions, translating this knowledge into load limits for a specific assembly remains difficult. This paper presents SAGE (Source-grounded Assembly Gating and Execution), a system that converts documented material evidence into capacity estimates for robotic insertion. SAGE restricts a large language model (LLM) to extracting tensile and compressive strengths from retrieved passages and tables and records their sources. A response model then interpolates offline finite element (FE) solutions to convert these strengths and the assembly conditions into axial load capacity. For fits with positive clearance, the estimated capacity sets the policy's axial force limit; for interference fits, it is compared with measured support demand to determine admission. On the primary benchmark, SAGE reduces mean capacity error from 80.65\% for direct LLM estimates based on the same evidence to 10.74\%. Without refitting, the mean error remains 8.00\% on 16 additional geometries. Under the assigned support release model, SAGE correctly classifies 59 of 62 scored simulation runs, with only conservative errors. In recorded xArm6 demonstrations, SAGE takes material documents as input and completes physical insertion in 9 of 13 trials. These results show that assigning document interpretation to the LLM and force calculation to an explicit mechanical model produces accurate capacity estimates and traceable insertion decisions.

</details>


### [24] [HIGenNTO: Scalable Humanoid Interaction Generation via Noise-Space Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22611)
*Lalit Jayanti,Kashu Yamazaki,Yuto Shibata,Kotaro Amaya,Katerina Fragkiadaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HIGenNTO：通过优化预训练文本条件运动模型的初始噪声，在稀疏时空与场景约束下合成人形机器人-场景交互运动参考，实现接触丰富任务的生成与部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人可通过模仿运动学参考习得复杂技能，但接触丰富交互的可靠参考难以获取：运动捕捉在遮挡与紧密物理接触下退化，重定向还会引入额外的接触与几何不一致问题。

Method: 提出 HIGenNTO 框架，在稀疏时空与场景约束下优化预训练文本条件运动模型的初始噪声。统一的目标函数同时满足期望接触、避免碰撞并维持稳定支撑，同时保留先验的真实性与时序连贯性；支持从零生成交互运动，并以分阶段方式组合长时程行为。

Result: 在机器人-环境与机器人-物体任务中，HIGenNTO 生成的运动可由仿真中的跟踪策略执行，也可用于训练仅依赖机载传感的深度条件视觉运动策略。在 Unitree G1 上部署了四项接触丰富任务。任务规格还可由编码智能体撰写，自动提出交互任务并编译为提示、约束与场景程序，撰写了八个评测任务中的三个及另外四种行为。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从高层任务描述到物理可执行人形交互的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots can acquire complex skills by imitating kinematic humanoid motion references, yet reliable references for contact-rich interactions remain difficult to obtain: motion capture deteriorates under occlusion and close physical contact, while retargeting introduces additional contact and geometric inconsistencies. We present HIGenNTO, a framework that synthesizes humanoid-scene interaction motion references by optimizing the initial noise of a pretrained text-conditioned motion model under sparse spatiotemporal and scene constraints. The same formulation satisfies desired contacts, avoids collisions, and maintains stable support while retaining the prior's realism and temporal coherence, generating interaction motions from scratch and composing long-horizon behaviors stage-wise. Across robot-environment and robot-object tasks, HIGenNTO produces motions that can be executed by tracking policies in simulation and used to train depth-conditioned visuomotor policies operating solely from onboard sensing. We deploy these policies on a Unitree G1 across four contact-rich tasks. Finally, the task specifications themselves can be written by a coding agent, which proposes interaction tasks and compiles them into prompt, constraint, and scene programs, authoring three of our eight evaluated tasks and four further behaviors. Together, these results establish a scalable path from high-level task descriptions to physically executable humanoid interactions.

</details>


### [25] [Simultaneous Forward and Inverse Human-in-the-Loop Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22630)
*Kyeongwon Park,Steven H. Collins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SFIHILO方法，通过前向-逆向同步的人机在环优化，从人类偏好中高效推断个体奖励函数并优化控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中主观体验重要，但用户所重视的结果及其在个体与情境间的差异常未知。逆向学习虽可利用人类数据识别用户奖励，但在辅助场景下，执行控制策略、测量生物力学/生理结果及收集反馈的实验成本高，限制了查询与优化迭代次数。

Method: SFIHILO同步进行前向与逆向学习：先用前向模型预测控制策略产生的用户结果，利用该模型主动查询以加速逆向奖励学习，随后在无额外用户试验的情况下优化最终控制策略；通过评估候选策略在前向与逆向信念上的不确定性减少量，并以区域偏好边界为目标进行打分。

Result: 仿真显示，SFIHILO在用户异质性、结果维度、精度要求、噪声水平及非平稳性条件下均有效；相比互信息方法，其主动查询策略显著提升逆向学习的样本效率，同时保持前向模型精度。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了推断潜在人类目标的能力，可实现更具可迁移性与有效性的人机交互。

Abstract: Subjective user experience is important to human-robot interaction, but the outcomes users value, and how those preferences vary across individuals and contexts, are often unknown. While inverse learning approaches using human data can help identify user rewards, in many assistive settings the experimental costs of executing a control policy, measuring biomechanical or physiological outcomes, and collecting user feedback often limit the number of queries and optimization iterations. Here, we present Simultaneous Forward and Inverse Human-In-the-Loop Optimization (SFIHILO), which efficiently infers individual-specific reward functions from preferences over human outcomes and identifies a final control policy that maximizes the learned reward. SFIHILO bootstraps a forward model to predict user outcomes from control policies, uses this model for active querying to accelerate inverse reward learning, and then optimizes the final control policy without additional user trials. We score candidate policies by their expected reduction in uncertainty across both forward and inverse beliefs, targeting a regional preference boundary to robustly inform this simultaneous learning process. In simulation, we show that SFIHILO was effective across user heterogeneity, outcome dimensionalities, precision requirements, noise levels, and nonstationarity; compared with mutual information approaches, the proposed active querying strategy significantly improved sample efficiency in inverse learning while preserving forward model accuracy. This approach demonstrates the potential to infer latent human goals, enabling more transferable and effective human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [26] [Density-Driven Area Coverage for Nonholonomic Multi-Robot Systems with Safety Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22668)
*Julian Martinez,Kooktae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将密度驱动最优控制(D2OC)与控制屏障函数(CBF)安全滤波器结合的方法，通过在物理输入层面直接施加安全约束，消除非完整机器人密度覆盖中安全认证与实际运动之间的不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D2OC可为多机器人团队分配非均匀空间分布，但当应用于非完整机器人时，安全约束施加在参考运动上，而实际机器人运动由物理输入决定，二者之间存在不一致（即参考运动安全并不保证物理间隙安全）。

Method: 将D2OC与CBF安全滤波器结合，通过反馈线性化的前视点（look-ahead point）将安全约束直接施加在机器人的物理输入上，使安全与执行器限制可在控制过程中一并考虑；并推导了考虑前视几何、机器人足迹及每个控制周期内运动的安全裕度。

Result: 仿真表明该方法在保持所需物理间距的同时，覆盖性能与常规参考跟踪方法相当（后者虽满足参考运动安全，却违反相应物理间隙）；在Robotarium上多台非完整机器人实验进一步验证了在驶向目标空间分布过程中的安全执行。

Conclusion: 在物理输入层面直接施加安全约束，可消除密度驱动多机器人覆盖中安全认证与物理机器人运动之间的不匹配。

Abstract: Density-Driven Optimal Control (D2OC) provides a principled approach to distributing multi-robot teams over non-uniform spatial distributions. Applying D2OC to nonholonomic robots, however, creates a gap between safety constraints imposed on a reference motion and the physical inputs that determine the actual robot motion. We address this issue by enforcing the safety constraint directly on the robot's physical inputs while preserving the density-driven coverage objective. The proposed framework combines D2OC with a control barrier function safety filter through a feedback-linearizing look-ahead point, allowing safety and actuator limits to be considered together during control. We further derive a safety margin that accounts for the look-ahead geometry, robot footprint, and motion during each control interval. Simulation results show that the proposed method maintains the required physical separation while achieving coverage performance comparable to a conventional reference-tracking approach, which can satisfy safety on the reference motion yet violate the corresponding physical clearance. Experiments on multiple nonholonomic robots in the Robotarium further demonstrate safe execution while driving the robots toward the desired spatial distribution. These results show that enforcing safety directly on the physical inputs can eliminate the mismatch between safety certification and physical robot motion in density-driven multi-robot coverage.

</details>


### [27] [AquaWorld: Structure-Consistent Underwater World Generation for Robot Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22670)
*Bin Peng,Tiandong Zhang,Ruidong Wang,Min Luo,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AquaWorld：一种以共享地形结构为核心的水下世界生成框架，实现地形、场景、任务与洋流的相互一致，并在流场预测与机器人学习上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人仿真需要多样环境，但地形、场景构成、任务与洋流之间必须保持相互一致；现有独立随机化方法难以维持这种跨因素关联，导致仿真环境不真实且不利于策略学习与迁移。

Method: 提出AquaWorld世界生成框架：以共享地形结构为纽带，用语言条件化的规划生成3D地形及其共享结构参考，进而引导资产放置、任务定义与入流设定，并在随机变化下保持一致性；集成超10,000个水下兼容资产，采用CFD监督的残差模型预测可复用的地形条件平均流场，支持常规水下航行器与仿生机器鱼。

Result: 在24组配对地形上，结构一致随机化的跨因素一致性显著优于独立随机化；在等预算策略训练对比中，结构一致随机化的验证成功率比独立随机化高21%；在独立物理实验中，仿真训练的视觉导航策略在95%的物理水槽试验中成功，且无需更新感知或控制模块。

Conclusion: AquaWorld提供了一种实用途径，可在生成多样水下环境的同时保留流场仿真与机器人学习所需的结构关系。

Abstract: Underwater robot simulation requires diverse environments in which terrain, scene composition, tasks, and currents remain mutually consistent. We present AquaWorld, a world-generation framework that preserves these relationships through shared terrain structure. A language-conditioned plan generates 3D terrain and shared structural references that guide asset placement, task definition, and inflow specification under stochastic variation. The framework incorporates over 10,000 underwater-compatible assets, predicts reusable terrain-conditioned mean-flow fields through a CFD-supervised residual model, and supports conventional underwater vehicles and bio-inspired robotic fish. On 24 paired terrains, structure-consistent randomization produces substantially better cross-factor consistency than independent randomization. In a matched-budget policy-training comparison, structurally coherent randomization achieves a validation success rate 21% higher than independent randomization. In separate physical experiments, a simulation-trained visual navigation policy succeeds in 95% of physical tank trials without updating its perception or control modules. Overall, AquaWorld provides a practical way to generate varied underwater environments while retaining the structural relationships needed for flow simulation and robot learning.

</details>


### [28] [SHAFT: A Slack-Compensating, Helical-Buckling-Attenuating Flexible-Shaft Transmission for Lightweight Multi-DoF Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22677)
*Tomoya Takahashi,Moses Gladson Selvamuthu,Richiro Tadakuma,Kazutoshi Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SHAFT柔性轴传动机构，通过近端张紧器补偿松弛、抑制螺旋屈曲，在不增加臂部运动质量的前提下，实现约30%效率提升和约65%角传动误差降低，并据此研制出280g的6自由度机械臂（含1自由度夹爪）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量化、细长机械臂适合人机共存环境下的安全作业。采用线驱动或Bowden cable等远端传动可将电机移至基座附近，从而降低惯量与臂径；但现有方案要么需增加滑轮等换向部件导致质量上升，要么因摩擦损失导致传动效率下降。柔性轴传动可同时规避质量增加与过大摩擦损失，却会因关节弯曲处产生松弛引发螺旋屈曲，进而使角传动误差随转角增大。因此需要一种兼顾轻量、高效与低传动误差的远端传动机构。

Method: 提出SHAFT（Slack-compensating, Helical-buckling-Attenuating Flexible-shaft Transmission）机构：通过近端张紧器补偿柔性轴传动中的松弛，抑制螺旋屈曲，从而在不增加臂段运动质量的情况下改善角传动误差与效率。基于该机构搭建6自由度机械臂及1自由度夹爪，采用含电机与张紧器的旋转模块，臂模块质量280g。

Result: 在包含四个90度弯折的传动路径中，所提机构相比无张紧器的柔性轴传动，效率提升约30%，角传动误差降低约65%。

Conclusion: SHAFT是一种新型远端驱动系统，可在不增加臂部运动质量的前提下实现轻量结构与高传动效率，有助于加速安全机器人在人类环境中的部署。

Abstract: Lightweight and slim manipulators enable safe operation in human living environments. Proximal actuation using remote transmission mechanisms, such as wire-driven or Bowden cables, effectively reduces inertia and arm size by relocating motors near the base and transmitting torque to distal joints. Existing approaches either increase mass through additional components, such as pulleys for direction changes, or suffer from reduced transmission efficiency due to friction losses. Flexible shaft transmission avoids both mass increase and excessive friction losses, but faces increasing angular transmission error due to helical buckling caused by slack generated at joint bending. To address this problem, we propose SHAFT: a Slack-compensating, Helical-buckling-Attenuating Flexible- shaft Transmission mechanism. This mechanism compensates for slack through a proximal tensioner, improving the angular transmission error and efficiency of flexible shaft transmission without increasing the moving mass of the arm section. In a transmission path containing four 90-degree bends, the proposed mechanism demonstrated approximately 30% higher efficiency and approximately 65% lower angular transmission error compared to a flexible shaft transmission without a tensioner. Using this mechanism, we fabricated a 6-Degree-of-Freedom (DoF) arm with a 1-DoF gripper manipulator consisting of a rotary module housing motors with a tensioner, and a 280 g weight for the arm module. The proposed manipulator represents a novel remote actuation system for achieving lightweight construction with high efficiency, contributing to the acceleration of safe robot deployment in human environments.

</details>


### [29] [A Direct Rigid Transmission 2-DoF Wrist Extension for Tendon-Driven Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22681)
*Yujie Pang,Sadman Sakib,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CRAFT-Wrist同心2自由度腕部扩展模块，在机器人手臂与CRAFT Hand之间安装，无需改动手部即可实现局部姿态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限空间中的灵巧操作需要手部姿态的局部控制；无腕部时，需通过机械臂多关节协同运动获得局部朝向，导致末端轨迹复杂。

Method: 设计同心2自由度腕部扩展，使用两个XC430-T240BB-T舵机经短刚性传动实现左右与前后旋转；在多种腕部姿态下表征手指-电机负载，并开展抓取、钉锤击打与黑板擦拭演示。

Result: 原型可在负载下驱动两轴，工作空间为径向-尺侧偏±20°（左右），前后范围为屈曲+80°、伸展−18°。

Conclusion: CRAFT-Wrist以低成本、不改动现有手部的模块化方式提供局部腕部姿态控制，适用于受限空间灵巧操作任务，并提供演示与装配说明。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation in confined spaces requires local control of hand orientation. Without a wrist, a dexterous hand must obtain this local orientation through coordinated motion of the robot arm, often involving several joints and a more complex end-effector path. We present CRAFT-Wrist, a concentric 2-DoF wrist extension that mounts between a robot arm and the CRAFT Hand without modifying the hand. Two XC430-T240BB-T servos drive sideways and front-back rotation through short rigid transmissions. Our initial hardware prototype actuates both axes under load, achieving a demonstrated workspace of $\pm20^{\circ}$ in radial--ulnar deviation (left--right) and a front-back range of $+80^{\circ}$ in flexion and $-18^{\circ}$ in extension. We characterize the resulting finger-motor loading across several wrist postures and demonstrate how the wrist supplies local orientation in grasping, nail hammering, and blackboard wiping. Demonstrations and assembly instructions are available at https://craft-wrist.github.io/.

</details>


### [30] [StateMem: Single-State Residual Memory with Adaptive Inference for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22684)
*Wenzhuo Li,Qiongfeng Shi,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出StateMem——一种用于VLA策略的单状态残差记忆框架，通过预测误差驱动低秩残差更新记忆令牌并自适应路由缓存前缀，在LIBERO、RoboMemArena及真实任务上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略主要依赖当前观测，历史信息保留能力有限；而以MemoryVLA为代表的记忆增强型VLA依赖外部记忆库，需显式存储与检索，带来额外开销与复杂度。

Method: 提出StateMem单状态残差记忆框架：利用预测误差，通过低秩残差更新一个持久记忆令牌，并自适应路由已缓存前缀；采用免训练控制器在线调整路由阈值，同时以快速校正补偿缓存复用时的过期前缀特征。

Result: 在LIBERO上平均成功率达97.6%，相对全量刷新降低VLM前缀平均刷新率20.25%；在RoboMemArena的Occlusion类别中，以21.8%任务成功率（TSR）和44.3%累积成功率（CSR）取得单VLA方法中的最佳表现；在六项真实操作任务上平均成功率提升21%。

Conclusion: StateMem以单状态残差记忆替代外部记忆库，在保持甚至提升操作成功率的同时显著降低前缀刷新开销，验证了其在记忆依赖型机器人操作任务中的有效性与效率优势。

Abstract: Memory-dependent robotic manipulation often requires later actions to use information from earlier interactions. Existing vision-language-action (VLA) policies primarily rely on current observations, limiting historical information retention. Memory-augmented VLAs, such as MemoryVLA, address this limitation with external memory banks but require explicit storage and retrieval. To address these limitations, we propose StateMem, a single-state residual memory framework for VLA policies that uses prediction error to update a persistent memory token through low-rank residuals and to adaptively route cached prefixes. A training-free controller adjusts the routing threshold online, while fast correction compensates for stale prefix features during cache reuse. We evaluate StateMem on LIBERO, RoboMemArena, and real-world manipulation tasks. On LIBERO, StateMem achieves an average success rate of 97.6% and reduces the average VLM prefix refresh rate by 20.25% relative to full refresh. In the Occlusion category of RoboMemArena, StateMem achieves the best performance among single-VLA methods, reaching 21.8% Task Success Rate (TSR) and 44.3% Cumulative Success Rate (CSR). Across six real-world manipulation tasks, it achieves +21% in average success rate.

</details>


### [31] [Decentralized Multi-Robot Exploration with Probabilistic Peer Intent and Multi-hop Plan Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22726)
*Saurbh Singh Jamwal,Nived Chebrolu,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出概率同伴意图（PPI）与多跳传播两种机制，改通信受限下的去中心化多机器人探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化多机器人探索中，通信受限下的协调仍具挑战。集中式方法依赖全局信息共享，在大规模或通信受限环境下不实用。现有基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的方法（如DMCE）通过交换规划路径序列实现去中心化规划，但同伴意图仅限于直接通信范围内的机器人。

Method: 在DMCE基础上提出概率同伴意图（PPI）：将同伴轨迹转换为连续的意图空间表示，并纳入局部MCTS的动作评估；同时研究通过多跳传播将同伴意图共享至直接通信范围之外的机制。

Result: 在多组仿真环境与不同团队规模下的实验表明，PPI与多跳传播均可提升去中心化探索性能，二者的相对收益取决于环境结构与团队规模；并在三类不同环境中实现了三台机器人的真实部署验证。

Conclusion: 将同伴意图表示为连续空间概率分布并适度扩展意图传播范围，能有效改善通信受限下的去中心化探索协调，且其收益与环境结构、团队规模相关。

Abstract: Efficient coordination under limited communication remains a key challenge in decentralized multi-robot exploration. While centralized approaches benefit from global information sharing, they are often impractical in large-scale or communication-constrained environments. Existing Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-based approaches, such as Decentralized Monte Carlo Exploration (DMCE), enable decentralized planning by taking peer intent into account. This peer intent is obtained by communicating sequences of planned waypoints with robots within direct communication range. In this work, we extend this idea by introducing Probabilistic Peer Intent (PPI), which converts peer trajectories into a continuous spatial representation of predicted intent and incorporates it into local MCTS action evaluation. We additionally study the effects of sharing peer intent beyond direct communication range by propagating plans over multiple hops. Experiments across multiple simulated environments and team sizes show that PPI and Multi-hop propagation can each improve decentralized exploration, with their relative benefits depending on environment structure and team size. We also demonstrate the real-world deployment of our method on three robots operating in different environment types.

</details>


### [32] [BEACON: Belief-Enabled Adaptive CONtrol for Imitation Learning under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22730)
*Moonyoung Lee,Soumojit Bhattacharya,George Kantor,Oliver Kroemer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将扩散策略条件化于基于贝叶斯信念的结构化隐状态表征（含当前状态估计与剩余不确定性），可解决模仿学习中观测混叠问题，实现历史感知的隐状态消歧，并隐式调节探索与利用行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作任务中存在无法直接观测的隐状态，需通过序列物理交互推断。在部分可观测设定下，直接用近期原始观测历史作为模仿学习策略的条件会导致状态混叠：相同观测可能源自不同隐状态，使策略对同一输入接收矛盾的动作标签，性能下降。

Method: 提出将扩散策略条件化于隐状态的贝叶斯信念结构化表征，该表征同时给出当前最可能的状态估计与剩余不确定性。以此结构化、紧凑的输入替代原始观测历史，使策略能依据信念不确定性隐式地在探索与利用行为之间调节，无需显式模式切换或奖励塑形。

Result: 在两个信念表征性质不同的领域中评估：基于触觉传感的玉米秸秆夹爪对准（连续信念），以及闭锁门开启（离散类别分布）。两个领域中，信念条件化策略均显著优于仅用观测的基线，并接近特权真值性能；消融实验表明策略在推理时会依据信念不确定性调整探索行为。

Conclusion: 以贝叶斯信念作为扩散策略的结构化条件输入，可有效实现历史感知的隐状态消歧，使策略在部分可观测操作任务中获得接近特权真值的性能，并具备基于不确定性的自适应探索能力。

Abstract: Robot manipulation tasks often involve hidden state information that cannot be directly observed and must be inferred through sequential physical interactions. In such partially observable settings, conditioning an imitation learning policy directly on the recent raw observation history leads to poor performance. This is due to state aliasing, wherein identical observations may arise from different hidden states, and the policy receives conflicting action labels for the same input. To enable history-aware disambiguation capability, we propose conditioning a diffusion policy on a structured representation of the hidden states using Bayesian belief that exposes both the current most likely state estimate and the remaining uncertainty. This representation replaces raw history with a structured, compact input, enabling the policy to implicitly modulate between exploratory and exploitative behaviors based on belief uncertainty, without explicit mode switching or reward shaping. We evaluate across two domains with qualitatively different belief representations: a continuous belief for cornstalk gripper alignment via tactile sensing, and a discrete categorical distribution for latched door opening. In both domains, the belief-conditioned policy substantially outperforms the observation-only baseline and approaches privileged ground-truth performance, with ablations illustrating that the policy adapts its exploration behavior depending on the belief uncertainty at inference time.

</details>


### [33] [Rapidly-Iterating Grid-Based Near-Optimal Kinodynamic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22733)
*Michael Moncton,Eric Frew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RDG算法，基于状态空间网格分解实现常数时间复杂度的节点选择与图修订，具备渐近近优性与概率完备性，在10自由度动运动学规划中平均解质量超越SST和DIRT达104%和40%，成功率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动运动学（kinodynamic）运动规划中，基于最近邻搜索的方法在生成探索偏置时效率受限，且现有具备最优性保证的稀疏算法（如SST、DIRT）在高自由度复杂环境下解质量与成功率不足。

Method: 提出快速迭代动运动学网格（RDG）算法：利用状态空间网格分解完成节点选择、动力学传播与图修订，使其时间复杂性相对轨迹树节点数保持常数；并通过覆盖球序列归纳证明其渐近近优性与概率完备性；同时对各子系统与常规最近邻搜索方法生成探索偏置的效果进行对比评估。

Result: 在最高10自由度的复杂动运动学规划仿真环境中，RDG的平均最终解质量分别比SST和DIRT提升最高104%和40%；在SST与DIRT均失败的10自由度测试用例上，RDG仍保持100%成功率。

Conclusion: RDG通过网格分解实现快速迭代，在保证渐近近优性和概率完备性的同时，显著优于SST与DIRT的解质量与成功率，适用于高自由度复杂动运动学运动规划任务。

Abstract: This paper develops the Rapidly-iterating kinoDynamic Grid (RDG) algorithm, an asymptotically near-optimal kinodynamic motion planning algorithm that produces high quality solutions through rapid iteration. The algorithm leverages a state space grid decomposition to perform node selection, dynamics propagation, and graph revision in constant time complexity with respect to the number of nodes in the trajectory tree. Through a covering ball sequence induction proof, the algorithm is shown to be asymptotically near-optimal and probabilistically complete. Different subsystems of the algorithm are evaluated against common nearest-neighbor search-based methods at generating exploration bias. The RDG algorithm is evaluated through simulated trials in complex, kinodynamic motion planning problem environments up to 10 DOF relative to similar sparse, kinodynamic planning algorithms with optimality guarantees, SST and DIRT. The RDG algorithm outperforms both SST and DIRT in mean final solution quality by up to 104% and 40% respectively. Additionally, RDG maintained a 100% success rate, even on a 10-DOF test case where both SST and DIRT did not.

</details>


### [34] [Distributed Infrastructure Sensors and Cues for Robotic Fire-Fighting and Safety System on a Lunar Base](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22786)
*Alexis Munguia,Eryc Rodriguez,Drake Russ,Jekan Thangavelautham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种"嵌入式智能"的月球栖息地火灾与应急响应安全技术：在舱内每几米贴上薄陶瓷二维码（每约2 m²一个），每个码载有本地楼层地图、传感器安全阈值与分步应急流程；低功耗漫游车数秒内解码并读取邻近温-气传感器簇，随即就地响应，无需全局地图。仿真显示快速灭火既简化任务，又避免资源浪费与不确定后果；知识分布于无源标牌上，损坏局部化、流程可通过更换单个码更新、算力需求极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期载人月球栖息地若发生火灾或有毒气体泄漏，需在数分钟内处置单个加压气瓶；而身着舱外服的航天员与存在通信延迟的地面支持无法保证这一响应速度，故需一种不依赖人力与地面遥控的快速应急机制。

Method: 将智能嵌入栖息地本体：在舱内地板/壁面每数米（约每2 m²）铺设薄陶瓷QR码，每码编码局部楼面图、传感器安全上限及分步危险处置预案；低功耗漫游车在数秒内解码标牌，轮询相邻温度-气体传感器簇，并依据本地信息立即执行动作，无需构建全局地图。

Result: 在代表性舱内布局的仿真中，该快速响应灭火方案既简化了任务执行，又避免了高昂的资源消耗与不确定的处置结果。

Conclusion: 由于知识分散存储于无源标牌，系统具有损伤局部化、通过更换单个码即可更新规程、算力需求极低等优势；但早期载人月球栖息地的快速响应火灾风险管理仍是待解决的重要挑战。

Abstract: There are renewed efforts to build a lunar base and house a team of astronauts on the Moon for extended periods. However, important challenges remain, such as a rapid-response fire-hazard management system. In the event of a fire or toxic leak inside an early crewed lunar habitat, a single pressurized cylinder must be handled within minutes. However, suited astronauts and radio-delayed support from Earth cannot guarantee such response times. We describe a prototype fire-fighting and emergency response safety technology that embeds intelligence in the habitat itself: using thin ceramic QR codes affixed every few meters, each carries a local floor map, safe sensor limits, and step-by-step hazard responses. A low-power rover decodes a plate in a matter of seconds, polls the adjoining temperature-gas sensor cluster, and acts immediately, eliminating the need for a global map . In a representative layout, the interior is divided into roughly one code per two square meters. Simulations show that rapid-response firefighting both simplifies the task and avoids costly resource use and indeterminate outcomes. Because knowledge is spread across passive plates, damage is localized, procedures can be updated by replacing a single code, and processor demands remain minimal. However, important challenges remain, such as a rapid-response fire-hazard management system. In the event of a fire or toxic leak inside an early crewed lunar habitat, a single pressurized cylinder must be handled within minutes. However, suited astronauts and radio-delayed support from Earth cannot guarantee such response times. We describe a prototype fire-fighting and emergency response safety technology that embeds intelligence in the habitat itself: using thin ceramic QR codes affixed every few meters, each carries a local floor map, safe sensor limits, and step-by-step hazard responses.

</details>


### [35] [Task-Oriented Co-Design and Optimization of Geared Actuators for Robotic Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22795)
*Xuanyu Huang,Jianqiang Dong,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向任务的执行器硬件-控制协同设计优化框架，结合代理模型与分层混合变量优化，在二自由度跳跃腿上验证，实现35.7 N·m/kg额定输出扭矩密度与更优跳跃性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同任务对执行器的扭矩与速度需求各异，而现有执行器多在部件层面针对扭矩或功率密度优化，缺乏任务导向；跨电机、减速器、传感器的系统级优化因机电-电磁耦合与高计算成本而困难，导致单部件改进未必提升特定任务下的机器人性能。

Method: 采用代理模型加速电机评估并支持耦合设计空间的全局探索；使用分层混合变量优化策略，结合离散枚举与尺寸、实值指标的连续搜索，通过取整选择剩余离散变量的可行值；在该搜索中联合优化额定输出扭矩密度与任务性能，并为每个硬件候选确定Bezier参数化的关节扭矩轨迹；最后通过执行器制造与二自由度跳跃腿实验进行验证。

Result: 基于实测质量，制造样机额定输出扭矩密度达35.7 N·m/kg，较广泛使用的商用齿轮关节执行器高约60%，且轻18.6%；在匹配台架条件下，以两倍额定扭矩实现跳跃高度提升12.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明该框架提供了从任务需求到执行器设计与控制的系统化路径，任务导向协同设计可同时提升扭矩密度与任务性能。

Abstract: Different tasks performed by legged robots impose distinct torque and speed requirements on actuators. Existing robotic actuators are generally optimized at the component level for metrics such as torque or power density, without explicit task guidance.
