Motivation: 在部分可观测的分散式多机器人团队中，决定某机器人下一步动作的关键信息往往只有队友可见。现有分散式方法只传递位置、规划轨迹等运动学信息，无法表达队友的感知内容；MARL 学习式通信虽可携带感知内容，但消息与任务耦合且不可解释。

Method: 每个机器人广播其自身感知流水线中已计算出的感知潜向量（自监督联合嵌入预测目标训练、训练后冻结、团队共享的编码器输出）；接收方仅通过任务奖励学习如何在行动中使用该消息。设计内容受控协议：固定带宽、延迟、拓扑与接收方，仅改变消息内容。

Result: 广播潜向量使导航者在 99.7% 的回合中避开被遮挡的危险物，与无噪声手工设计消息持平；位置与轨迹消息仅相当于随机水平；宽度为潜向量 186 倍的原始相机图像可靠性更低。结论从离散网格世界扩展到连续速度控制下的渲染像素；编码器在真机摄像头 102/102 次实时决策中成功解码危险物。

Conclusion: 复用冻结的感知潜向量作为通信内容，可零额外计算、极低带宽地传递感知信息，且因其在任何策略训练前冻结而具备团队一致的语义、无需接收方知晓其含义。同时指出将 MARL 通信结果迁移到连续控制的一个前提：消息所支撑的决策必须仍可由探索到达，并给出恢复方法。

Abstract: In a decentralized multi-robot team under partial observability, the fact that decides a robot's next action is often visible only to a teammate. Existing decentralized methods communicate kinematic information, such as position or planned trajectory, which cannot convey what the teammate perceives. Learned communication in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) can carry perceptual content, but the resulting messages are task-coupled and opaque. We propose Latent Telepathy. Each robot broadcasts the perceptual latent vector it already computes for its own use, the output of an encoder trained with a self-supervised joint-embedding predictive objective, frozen, and shared across the team. A teammate learns to act on it from task reward alone. Because the encoder already runs for perception, the message costs no additional computation and a single compact vector of bandwidth. Because the encoder is frozen before any policy is trained, the message means the same thing to every robot, and the receiving robot is never told what it means. We evaluate Latent Telepathy with a content-controlled protocol in which bandwidth, latency, topology and receiver are held fixed and only the message content varies. Broadcasting the latent lets a navigator avoid an occluded hazard in 99.7% of episodes, matching a noiseless hand-designed message. Position and trajectory messages remain at chance, and the raw camera image, 186 times wider, is less reliable than the compressed latent. The result holds from a discrete gridworld to rendered pixels under continuous velocity control, and the encoder decodes the hazard from a physical robot's camera in 102 of 102 live decisions. We also identify a requirement for porting MARL communication results to continuous control, that the decision a message informs must remain reachable by exploration, and show how to restore it.

</details>


### [71] [Steering Through Contact: A Finite-Support Motion Model for Single-Track Center-Articulated Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23271)
*Mohamed Dhia Ounally,Nicolas Samson,Mathis Turgeon-Roy,Veronica Vannini,Johann Laconte,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出有限支撑二次模型（FSQ），通过准静态侧滑平衡刻画沿接触分布的旋转阻力，推广了点接触运动学模型（PCK），并给出实时预测横向速度与横摆角速度的闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场地机器人轨迹规划与控制依赖对推进和转向在接触点打滑时如何影响车辆运动的预测。铰接式车辆常用的点接触运动学模型（PCK）考虑了连杆几何，却忽略了沿接触分布的旋转阻力，导致预测精度受限。

Method: 针对单轨、中心铰接车辆提出有限支撑二次模型（FSQ），通过侧向滑移的准静态平衡引入分布式旋转阻力，放宽接触点假设以推广标准PCK；并将二次滑移代价精确归约为接触力矩，得到用于实时预测横向速度与横摆角速度的紧凑闭式解。

Result: 在沥青、草地、冰面及混合路线的真实实验中，使用超过7 km数据进行评估。对于5秒预测，FSQ相较PCK将加权中位平移误差和横摆误差分别降低53.5%和68.9%。

Conclusion: FSQ在保持实时性的同时显著提升铰接式车辆在滑移条件下的运动预测精度，优于忽略分布式旋转阻力的PCK模型。

Abstract: Trajectory planning and control in field robotics rely on predicting how propulsion and steering affect vehicle motion when contact points undergo slip. For articulated vehicles, the point-contact kinematic model (PCK) accounts for the linkage geometry but neglects the rotational resistance distributed along the contacts. We propose a finite-support quadratic model (FSQ) for single-track, center-articulated vehicles that incorporates this resistance through a quasi-static balance of lateral slip. Our approach generalizes the standard PCK formulation by relaxing the contact-point assumption. An exact reduction of the quadratic slip cost to contact moments gives a compact closed-form solution for real-time prediction of lateral velocity and yaw rate. We evaluate the proposed method in real-world experiments across asphalt, grass, ice, and mixed routes, using more than 7 km of data. For five-second predictions, FSQ reduces the weighted median translation and yaw errors by 53.5% and 68.9%, respectively, relative to PCK.

</details>


### [72] [SCULPT-VLA: Learning Structured Control through Staged Action Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23275)
*Wenbo Li,Yiteng Chen,Wei Zhang,Wenhao Li,Jun Yang,Qingyao Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCULPT-VLA 提出分阶段动作接地训练框架，先借助教师支架学习任务进展、场景动态、空间接地三类结构化中间因素，再逐步撤除支架、恢复感知细节，实现无需教师与离散动作自回归的 VLA 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 策略引入结构化中间监督，但"中间表示应编码什么"与"动作预测如何学会依赖它"是两个独立问题，后者常被忽视。作者希望显式分离"基于结构条件化动作"与"精炼连续控制"这两个学习目标。

Method: SCULPT-VLA 的动作条件状态由任务进展、场景动态、空间接地三类互补因素构成。训练分阶段：先用教师支架形成这些因素，再以其组合接地粗略动作预测并逐步撤除支架，最后恢复直接感知访问以结合所学状态与感知细节进行连续精炼。部署时不需要教师，也不需要离散动作自回归。

Result: 在 LIBERO、SimplerEnv-WidowX、RoboTwin 2.0 Full 上平均成功率高于共享骨干基线。SimplerEnv-WidowX 最终成功率 83.5%，而 Stage-II 动作学习直接访问视觉语言时为 71.3%。四个真实机器人任务在测试分布偏移下平均成功率 58.1%，对比 π_{0.5} 的 45.6%。训练消融与因素级干预支持分阶段设计，并表明恢复直接感知后所学状态仍对控制有贡献。

Conclusion: 分阶段动作接地能有效将结构化中间表示转化为动作预测的依赖，且所学结构化状态在感知访问恢复后仍持续贡献于控制，验证了课程式设计在 VLA 策略中的价值。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies increasingly incorporate structured intermediate supervision beyond action labels. Yet specifying what an intermediate representation should encode leaves open how action prediction learns to depend on it. We introduce \textbf{SCULPT-VLA}, a policy that learns structured control through staged action grounding. Its action-conditioning state comprises complementary factors for task progression, scene dynamics, and spatial grounding. Training first forms these factors with teacher scaffolds, then grounds coarse action prediction through their composition as scaffold inputs are withdrawn. Direct perceptual access is subsequently restored for continuous refinement, combining the learned state with perceptual detail. The curriculum separates learning to condition actions on structure from refining continuous control. Deployment requires neither teachers nor discrete-action autoregression. SCULPT-VLA achieves higher average success than shared-backbone baselines on LIBERO, SimplerEnv-WidowX, and RoboTwin 2.0 Full. On SimplerEnv-WidowX, final success is 83.5\%, versus 71.3\% when Stage-II action learning directly accesses vision and language. Across four physical robot tasks, average success under the tested distribution shifts reaches 58.1\%, compared with 45.6\% for $π_{0.5}$. Training ablations and factor-wise interventions support the staged design and show that the learned state continues to contribute to control after direct perceptual access is restored.

</details>


### [73] [Safety-Critical Control under Uncertainty via Adaptive Conformal Quantile Prediction Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23280)
*Hao Zhou,Yanze Zhang,Yiwei Lyu,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自适应共形预测与共形分位数回归的安全控制框架，构建标定且状态相关的不确定性区间，并嵌入概率控制障碍函数与MPC，实现高概率安全保证并降低保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性下的安全关键控制需要既统计有效又可执行的安全约束。现有方法常假设特定分布以证明安全性，或建立对称且与输入无关的预测区间，导致控制综合中的安全约束失配或过度保守。

Method: 利用自适应共形预测（ACP）并扩展共形分位数回归（CQR），构造无分布假设、非对称的不确定性区间，具有可证明的概率覆盖率；将所得不确定性集合融入概率控制障碍函数（CBF）表述以强制鲁棒安全，并嵌入模型预测控制（MPC）框架形成不确定性感知的安全约束。

Result: 仿真与理论结果验证了该方法在提供高概率可证明安全行为的同时，能降低保守性并改善约束控制性能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过自适应不确定性量化实现了统计有效且状态相关的安全约束，为不确定性下的安全关键控制提供了兼顾可证明性与性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Safety-critical control under uncertainty requires uncertainty representations that are both statistically valid (for certifiable performance) and compatible with enforceable safety constraints. However, existing methods often assume particular distributions of uncertainty for provable safety guarantees or establish symmetric and input-agnostic prediction intervals for robust safety, which can lead to misaligned or overly conservative safety constraints in control synthesis. In this paper, we introduce a novel safe control framework with adaptive uncertainty quantification that constructs calibrated and state-dependent prediction intervals to enable high-probability safety guarantees, while improving constrained control performance. The framework leverages adaptive conformal prediction (ACP) and extends it with conformal quantile regression (CQR) to capture distribution-free, asymmetric uncertainty intervals with certifiable probabilistic coverage, and integrates the resulting uncertainty sets into a probabilistic control barrier function formulation to enforce robust safety with reduced conservativeness. This yields uncertainty-aware safe control constraints that can be incorporated within a model predictive control(MPC) framework to provide provably safe behaviors with high probability. Simulation and theoretical results are provided to demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [74] [Shared Execution-Clock Drifting Policy for Dynamic Precision Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23305)
*Zhenchen Dong,Qingran Wu,Jinna Fu,Jiaming Wu,Fulin Chen,Hongyu Yu,Yide Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SECD方法，将执行节奏显式建模为一步式动作生成中的共享单调时钟，与进度索引动作曲线联合预测，保留固定频率控制接口且仅需一次网络评估，在四项真机任务上取得77.00%平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间约束下的操作（如拦截移动物体、在截止时间前完成一系列调整）不仅需要准确的动作，还需要与场景演变相匹配的执行节奏。一步式策略虽降低了生成成本，但其直接预测的动作序列隐含了时间分配，缺乏对执行节奏的显式建模，难以应对动态时序要求。

Method: SECD以观测和隐变量采样为条件，联合预测进度索引的动作曲线与一个共享单调时钟，后者将固定控制时刻映射到曲线上的位置，使执行节奏成为一步式动作生成的显式组成部分。通过从示教数据中导出的对齐关系锚定该分解（即时钟与动作曲线的对齐），并以drift方式在解码动作上联合训练。策略保持固定频率控制接口，推理仅需一次网络评估。

Result: 在四项真机任务、NVIDIA Thor推理下进行300次试验，取得77.00%的任务平均成功率，并在每项任务上均优于所评估的一步式基线；其中16 m/min传送带上取杯成功率达91%，90秒内复原并折叠皱衬衫达54%。固定时钟变体在同一传送带协议上达到79%。基于状态的RoboMimic实验（含Transport与Square的跨随机种子消融）进一步支持时间表示与示教对齐的联合设计。

Conclusion: 将执行节奏作为显式的时间表示并借助示教对齐进行联合训练，可在保持一步式生成低延迟优势的同时提升时间约束操作的成功率，验证了时间表示与示教对齐联合设计的有效性。

Abstract: Manipulation under time constraints requires both accurate actions and an execution rhythm that matches the evolving scene. This becomes critical when a robot must intercept moving objects or complete a sequence of adjustments before a deadline. Although one-step policies reduce generation cost, their directly predicted action sequences leave temporal allocation implicit. We propose Shared Execution-Clock Drifting (SECD), which makes execution rhythm an explicit part of one-step action generation. Conditioned on an observation and a latent sample, the policy jointly predicts a progress-indexed action curve and a shared monotone clock that maps fixed control times to locations on the curve. Demonstration-derived alignment anchors this decomposition, which is trained jointly through drifting on the decoded actions. The resulting policy retains a fixed-rate control interface and requires one network evaluation. We evaluate SECD across four real-robot tasks with inference on NVIDIA Thor. Across 300 trials, it achieves 77.00% task-averaged success and outperforms the evaluated one-step baselines on every task, including 91% success in cup retrieval from a 16 m/min conveyor and 54% in restoring and folding a crumpled shirt within 90 s. A fixed-clock variant reaches 79% on the same conveyor protocol. Complementary state-based RoboMimic experiments, including cross-seed ablations on Transport and Square, further support the joint design of the temporal representation and demonstration alignment. Project page: https://secd-anonymous-ewn.pages.dev/

</details>


### [75] [Manipulation Feasible Navigation Among Movable Obstacles with Discrete Contact Pushing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23312)
*Shaohu Wang,Aiguo Song,Yulong Yuan,Zhongyu Sun,Tianyuan Miao,Qinjie Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向移动机械臂的分层NAMO框架，结合高层重定位规划、LLM辅助依赖推理与RL低层推动控制，实现大型不可抓取障碍物的交互式导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在大型可移动障碍物的环境中，仅靠绕行导航效率低甚至不可行，而直接交互障碍物又需要同时推理导航收益、可行放置位置与可执行操作，缺乏统一且可验证的解决方案。

Method: 分层框架：高层从参考路径中识别关键阻塞障碍物，搜索同时满足几何、操作与下游导航约束的重定位方案；当直接重定位受阻时，选择性调用LLM推断辅助操作依赖，并由确定性几何规划验证；执行层在箱形障碍物表面定义离散接触模式，依据位姿误差在线选择接触面与区域，通过接触切换实现直推、侧推与角推；采用循环强化学习策略协同移动底盘与机械臂跟踪TCP目标并保持末端可达性。

Result: 仿真与真实机器人实验表明，在绕行、单障碍与多障碍重定位以及依赖约束场景中均能实现可行的导航—操作，验证了框架对大型不可抓取障碍物的交互式导航能力。

Conclusion: 该分层NAMO框架通过几何验证与LLM推理结合、离散接触模式推动与RL协同控制，为移动机械臂在复杂可移动障碍环境中的交互式导航提供了可行方案，并已开源。

Abstract: In environments with large movable obstacles, detour-only navigation can be inefficient or even infeasible, while obstacle interaction requires reasoning about navigation benefit, feasible placement, and executable manipulation. We present a hierarchical navigation among movable obstacles (NAMO) framework for mobile manipulators. At the high level, the planner identifies key blocking obstacles from reference paths and searches for relocation plans that jointly satisfy geometric, manipulation, and downstream navigation constraints. When direct relocation is hindered by other movable objects, a large language model (LLM) is selectively invoked to infer auxiliary manipulation dependencies, which are then verified by deterministic geometric planning. To execute the resulting relocation goals, we define discrete contact modes on the surfaces of box-shaped obstacles and select contact faces and regions online based on position and orientation errors, enabling straight, side, and corner pushing through contact switching. A recurrent reinforcement-learning policy coordinates the mobile base and manipulator to track tool center point (TCP) targets while preserving end-effector reachability during sustained pushing. Simulation and real-robot experiments demonstrate feasible navigation-manipulation in detour, single- and multi-obstacle relocation, and dependency-constrained scenarios, validating the framework for interactive navigation with large non-graspable obstacles. The open-source project is available at https://cloudytosunny.github.io/NAMO_DCPushing/.

</details>


### [76] [Towards Reliable Underwater Diver-Robot Interaction: Gesture Design, Interaction Logic, and Real-World Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23392)
*Yingqi Liu,Kanzhong Yao,Zimeng Peng,Yuanbo Bi,Anran Li,Zhe Sun,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个闭环的水下人-机器人交互框架，集成了7种手势词汇、轻量级基于关键点的识别和命令级交互逻辑，并通过用户研究与水下机器人实验验证其可用性、可识别性与执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下人-机器人交互需要既便于潜水员使用、又能被机器人可靠识别的手势命令。论文旨在从人类可用性、手势可识别性与执行可靠性三方面综合探究这一交互问题。

Method: 构建闭环潜水员-机器人交互框架，包含紧凑的七手势词汇、轻量级基于关键点的识别方法，以及命令级交互逻辑；通过用户研究和在实验室水箱与游泳池中的水下机器人实验进行评估。

Result: 用户研究表明手势经简短学习后具有可复现性；识别分析显示视觉相似性与手势混淆相关，而手势形成过程中的中间姿态引入时间歧义；命令级处理减轻了瞬时识别错误对机器人执行的影响，减少了误触发和任务过早中断。

Conclusion: 可靠的水下手势交互依赖于人类可用性、手势可识别性和水下交互中的执行可靠性三者的共同作用。

Abstract: Underwater human--robot interaction requires gesture commands that are both easy for divers to use and reliable for robots to recognize. We investigate these aspects through a closed-loop diver--robot interaction framework integrating a compact seven-gesture vocabulary, lightweight landmark-based recognition, and command-level interaction logic. We evaluate the framework through a user study and underwater robot experiments in a laboratory tank and a swimming pool. The user study supported the reproducibility of the gestures after brief learning. Recognition analysis further showed that visual similarity was associated with gesture confusion, while intermediate poses during gesture formation introduced temporal ambiguity. Command-level processing mitigated the effects of transient recognition errors on robot execution, reducing unintended triggers and premature task interruptions. These findings show that reliable underwater gesture interaction depends on human usability, gesture recognizability, and execution reliability in underwater interaction.

</details>


### [77] [EmoPose: Vision-Language Model Guided Emotion-Aware Gesture Generation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23414)
*Daojie Peng,Bingtao Wang,Fulong Ma,Wenjun Yue,Liang Zhang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmoPose 是一个由视觉语言模型（VLM）引导的框架，通过可执行的语义接口，让人形机器人既能表达社交化手势，又能保持确定性的、感知本体结构的运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会化的类人机器人需要通过手势和语音传递情感与意图，但开放式交互必须转化为既具表现力又能特
定身体上执行的运动。这要求在保留确定性、感知本体结构的机器人控制的同时，具备应对上下文社交意图的语义灵活性。

Method: 提出 EmoPose 框架：给定语言、对话历史与可选视觉上下文，VLM 选择有序手势计划（交际类别、库变体、强度、语音锚点）。机器人自有的可扩展运动库定义可用的表现词汇及 14 自由度关节目标来源；Pose Studio 支持自动轨迹生成、MuJoCo 预览，并将新库条目自动与 VLM 指南同步。机器人端确定性模块负责校验计划、构建轨迹、调度手势、管理队列与打断。

Result: 在 EmoPose-Bench 上，结构化规划在 Easy 层级达到 98.25±0.52%，整体 76.50±0.54%，优于同模型直接标注提示。进一步实验验证了对话上下文使用与有序多动作组合。系统完成标称 MuJoCo 套件，并在物理 Unitree G1 上实现全部 29 个编排变体；四站点实验室导览演示了带打断的表现性叙述、相机接地的对话与导航。

Conclusion: EmoPose 通过可执行语义接口在语义灵活性与确定性控制之间取得平衡，使交互词库可在不改变控制接口、不将原始关节命令交给基础模型的前提下，针对新社交场景扩展。

Abstract: Socially competent humanoid robots must communicate affect and intent through gesture as well as speech, yet open-ended interaction must become motion that is both expressive and executable on a specific body. This demands semantic flexibility for contextual social intent while preserving deterministic, embodiment-aware robot control. We present EmoPose, a vision-language model (VLM)-guided framework that bridges this gap through an executable semantic interface. Given language, dialogue history, and optional visual context, the VLM selects an ordered gesture plan containing a communicative class, library variant, intensity, and speech anchor. A scalable robot-owned motion library defines the available expressive vocabulary and the source of 14-DoF joint targets. Pose Studio supports automatic trajectory generation, MuJoCo preview, and automatic synchronization of new library entries with the VLM guide; deterministic robot-side modules validate plans, construct trajectories, schedule gestures, and manage queueing and interruption. This division lets the interaction repertoire grow for new social contexts without changing the control interface or delegating raw joint commands to the foundation model. On the EmoPose-Bench, structured GPT-5.5 planning reaches $98.25\pm0.52\%$ on the Easy tier and $76.50\pm0.54\%$ overall, exceeding same-model direct-label prompting. Further tests validate dialogue-context use and ordered multi-action composition. The system completes the nominal MuJoCo suite and realizes all 29 authored variants on the physical Unitree G1. A four-stop laboratory tour demonstrates expressive narration with interruption, camera-grounded dialogue, and navigation.

</details>


### [78] [HEARTH: An Object-Centric RGB-Thermal-3D Dataset for Temperature-Aware Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23418)
*Yuning Su,Borui Li,Yonghao Shi,Bofei Liu,Xing-Dong Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HEARTH：面向机器人学习的RGB-热成像-3D物体数据集，含90个物体、145个状态，用热观测微调VLA使温度相关任务成功率从35.0%提升至75.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言引导的操作可能依赖可见外观无法揭示的物理属性，温度即为典型例子；而现有机器人学习物体数据集很少将实测温度与物体外观和几何形状关联，导致策略难以遵循温度相关指令。

Method: 构建HEARTH物体中心数据集：18个日常类别、90个物理物体、145个捕获物体状态；通过相机标定与位姿迁移将表观表面温度映射到重建网格；提供原始温度测量、相机参数、RGB纹理网格与热纹理；基于LIBERO派生三个任务，采集1,200条演示，微调预训练VLA模型π0.5，并进行消融研究。

Result: 在温度依赖的物体选择任务中，将热观测加入VLA后成功率由RGB-only基线的35.0%提升至75.0%。

Conclusion: HEARTH可用于训练机器人策略遵循温度相关指令，证明热模态观测对温度依赖操作任务具有显著价值。

Abstract: Language-guided manipulation can depend on physical properties that visible appearance does not reveal. Temperature is one such property, but object datasets for robot learning rarely associate measured temperatures with object appearance and geometry. We present HEARTH, an object-centric RGB-thermal-3D dataset of 90 physical objects from 18 everyday categories, comprising 145 captured object states. Our pipeline maps apparent surface temperatures onto reconstructed meshes through camera calibration and pose transfer. The dataset includes raw temperature measurements, camera parameters, RGB-textured meshes, and thermal textures for simulation. We use these assets to construct three LIBERO-derived tasks and collect 1,200 demonstrations for fine-tuning a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model, $π_{0.5}$. In an ablation study, adding thermal observations to the VLA increases success on temperature-dependent object-selection tasks from 35.0% for the RGB-only baseline to 75.0%. These results demonstrate the utility of HEARTH for training robot policies to follow temperature-related instructions.

</details>


### [79] [RiverVLN: Phase-Grounded Temporal Vision--Language Navigation for Unmanned Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23423)
*Jieling Wu,Yuehao Huang,Jiajun Lv,Tao Huang,Yong Liu,Weiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向内河无人水面艇（USV）长时序视觉语言导航（VLN）的基准 RiverVLN 与框架 PGT-NAV，将整条指令转为有序可视觉验证的语义阶段，通过阶段锚定融合视觉-运动历史与局部 SE(2) 位姿增量预测，在闭环中显著降低漂移，平均成功率 0.79，并成功迁移至真实 USV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLN 主要面向室内与地面机器人，隐含"语言即静态目标、运动可近似为离散或近瞬时动作"的假设。对 USV 而言该假设失效：内河航行需在惯性与有限机动性下连续运动，且长时序指令须依赖稀疏、视觉歧义大的水上地标来执行。

Method: 提出 PGT-NAV 阶段锚定时序导航框架。不将完整指令直接映射为运动，而是将其转化为有序的、可视觉验证的语义阶段序列，并利用锚定的视觉与运动证据在线维持当前活跃阶段。该显式语义进度状态与视觉-运动历史及阶段特定锚定相融合，预测六个局部 SE(2) 位姿增量。轨迹在"预测-执行-再观测"循环中执行：艇体朝 W3 行进，更新阶段与锚定，并经基于地图的安全层重新规划。同时构建 RiverVLN 基准。

Result: 相比 GNM 类与 ViNT 类基线，PGT-NAV 显著降低递归的位置与航向漂移；在 Unity-ROS 闭环导航中平均成功率达 0.79；未见过的开桥场景试验及真实 USV 实验表明阶段锚定表示可从受控评测迁移至物理 USV 部署。

Conclusion: 阶段锚定的语义进度表示能有效应对连续内河运动下的长时序 USV 视觉语言导航，兼具闭环稳定性与从仿真到实艇的迁移能力，为水上 VLN 提供了新基准与可行框架。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) has largely been developed for indoor and terrestrial robots, where language can often be treated as a static goal and motion is approximated by discrete or near-instantaneous actions. These assumptions break down for unmanned surface vehicles (USVs): river navigation requires continuous motion under inertia and limited maneuverability, while long-horizon instructions must be executed through sparse and visually ambiguous maritime landmarks. We introduce RiverVLN, to our knowledge the first benchmark designed for long-horizon USV VLN under continuous riverine motion, and PGT-NAV, a phase-grounded temporal navigation framework for USVs. Rather than directly mapping an entire instruction to motion, PGT-NAV converts it into an ordered sequence of visually verifiable semantic phases and maintains the active phase online through grounded visual and motion evidence. This explicit semantic progress state is fused with visual-motion history and phase-specific grounding to predict six local SE(2) pose increments. The resulting trajectory is executed in a predict-execute-re-observe loop, where the vessel executes toward W3, updates phase and grounding, and replans through a map-based safety layer. Experiments show that PGT-NAV substantially reduces recursive position and heading drift relative to GNM-style and ViNT-style baselines and achieves an average success rate of 0.79 in Unity-ROS closed-loop navigation. Unseen bridge-opening trials and real-world USV experiments further demonstrate that the phase-grounded representation transfers from controlled evaluation to physical USV deployment.

</details>


### [80] [RopeFormer: Cross-Trial Adaptation from Interaction History for Dynamic Rope Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23432)
*Menglin Wu,Kaixiang Yao,Shangbo Luan,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Yuxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RopeFormer——一种以历史交互为条件的绳索动态操作框架，无需在线估计绳索物理参数，通过在跨试验中保留"动作-响应"历史上下文提升控制性能，并在仿真与 Unitree H1-2 实物上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态绳索操作对未知物体动力学高度敏感：相同机器人动作在不同绳索上会产生显著不同的响应，而显式辨识相关物理属性又十分困难。因此需要一种不依赖参数估计、能适应绳索差异的控制方式。

Method: 提出 RopeFormer：以先前任务交互作为后续控制的上下文，策略在保持权重固定的前提下保留跨试验的动作-响应历史，不进行显式在线绳索参数估计。评估涵盖持续单臂旋转、双臂旋转与瞬态甩绳三类任务，并在 Unitree H1-2 上用未见过的真实绳索部署冻结策略。

Result: 在匹配仿真对比中，保留上下文相比重置同一检查点在后续控制上均有提升，且收益随绳索动力学与观测设置而变化。实物部署中，从 T1 到 T3，Rope Swing 目标获取时间减少 30.9%，Rope Twirl 减少 33.9%；Rope Whip 平均目标命中数从 0.2 提升至 2.3（满分 3）。

Conclusion: 先前交互可为动态可变形物体操作提供有效的控制上下文，说明以历史为条件的策略能在不显式辨识参数的情况下适应未知绳索动力学，并具备从仿真到实物的迁移能力。

Abstract: Dynamic rope manipulation is highly sensitive to unknown object dynamics: the same robot motion can produce substantially different responses across ropes, while explicitly identifying the relevant physical properties is difficult. We present RopeFormer, a history-conditioned framework that uses prior task interaction as context for subsequent control. The policy retains cross-trial action-response history while keeping its weights fixed and requires no explicit online rope-parameter estimation. In matched simulation evaluations across sustained single-arm rotation, bimanual rotation, and transient whipping, retaining context improves subsequent control relative to resetting the same checkpoint, with the benefit varying across rope dynamics and observation settings. We further deploy the frozen policies on a Unitree H1-2 with previously unseen physical ropes. From T1 to T3, target-acquisition time decreases by 30.9% for Rope Swing and 33.9% for Rope Twirl, while mean Rope Whip target hits increase from 0.2 to 2.3 out of three. These results show that prior interaction can provide effective control context for dynamic deformable-object manipulation. Robot videos, code, and data are available at https://ropeformer.github.io/.

</details>


### [81] [Receding-Horizon Pushing with Composable Object-Centric Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23439)
*Zhiyi Yuan,Tianrun Hu,Anxing Xiao,Yuhong Deng,David Hsu,Hanbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种以物体为中心的分层推演策略，结合学习型接触预测、稳定性分析与BIT*反馈重规划，实现长时程任意形状3D物体的推动操作，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程推动任意形状3D物体面临三个耦合难题：如何选择接触点以逼近目标位姿、单次推动是否稳定可达、以及后续动作是否仍可行，现有方法难以同时解决。

Method: 在反馈引导的分层框架下构建物体中心推演策略。低层用学习型策略从位姿与尺度归一化的点云预测接触动作，并以近单步子目标为条件；用准静态滑动-倾覆分析计算稳定性分数评估接触。高层由BIT*先搜索物体路径，再用接触预测与机器人运动规划校验后续若干子目标的前瞻可行性；运动规划失败作为反馈修改局部路径代价并触发重规划。执行时仅执行首个可行动作。

Result: 在仿真中对6个场景下22个物体进行评估，并开展全面消融实验；结果表明方法以明显优势超过基线，能在多种情况下可靠完成长时程推动任务。同时在Franka机械臂上进行定量真机实验，并用移动操作平台对大型重物做定性演示，实现零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: 所提分层框架有效融合学习型接触预测、稳定性分析与反馈式重规划，可实现任意形状3D物体的长时程非抓取推动，并具备良好的仿真到现实泛化能力。

Abstract: Non-prehensile manipulation is practical for relocating large, heavy, or geometrically ungraspable objects. Yet, long-horizon pushing of arbitrarily-shaped 3D objects couples three problems: 1) where to push the object so as to approach the target pose, 2) whether each push is stable and reachable, 3) whether subsequent actions remain feasible. We present an object-centric pushing policy within a feedback-guided hierarchical framework. At the low level, a learning-based policy predicts contact actions from a pose- and scale-normalized point cloud, conditioned on a near single-step subgoal. A stability score is applied to evaluate the predicted contacts by a quasi-static sliding-versus-tipping analysis. At the high level, BIT$^*$ first searches for an object path, and the next several subgoals are checked by contact prediction and robot motion planning for future feasibility. Failed motion plans, as feedback, change the local path costs and trigger re-planning. During execution, only the first feasible action is executed. In simulation, we evaluate 22 objects in six different scenes, upon which we also conduct comprehensive ablation studies. Results demonstrate that our method outperforms baselines with a clear margin and can reliably achieve long-horizon object pushing tasks under different situations. We also report quantitative real-robot experiments with a Franka arm and qualitative demonstrations with a mobile manipulator for large and heavy objects, with directly zero-shot sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [82] [BiRoAD: Learning Shared and Role-Adaptive Representations for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23445)
*Yan Shen,Yuchen Liu,Feng Jiang,Hangtian Hu,Xiaoqi Li,Shu Chen,Ruihai Wu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BiRoAD框架，将双臂特征分解为交换对称与交换反对称分量，实现角色自适应表示学习，在角色分布不均衡时提升策略鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作需协调两臂并随场景几何、物体配置与任务上下文调整功能角色。现有学习面临两大挑战：一是示教数据中角色分布不均衡，导致对欠代表的手臂-角色配置泛化差；二是多数策略在固定的左/右臂动作空间中预测动作，虽便于控制，却未显式刻画功能角色在双臂间交换时行为应如何变换。

Method: 提出BiRoAD双臂角色自适应分解框架：输入双臂轨迹或动作token特征，将其分解为交换对称分量（捕捉对臂交换不变的协调结构）与交换反对称分量（捕捉随功能角色分配一致变化的角色特异性差异）；再将两分量重组为对原始成对臂表示的残差更新。该方法为模块化特征变换，不改变策略输入与模仿学习目标，也无需人工标注角色标签。

Result: 在多个双臂操作任务（角色分布均衡与不均衡）上，BiRoAD相较于对应基础策略提升了跨角色配置的鲁棒性，在欠代表的角色配置上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 通过交换对称/反对称分解实现共享与角色自适应表示，BiRoAD以即插即用的方式增强双臂策略的角色泛化能力，尤其缓解角色分布不均衡问题。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation requires policies that coordinate two arms while adapting their functional roles to scene geometry, object configuration, and task context. Learning such scene-conditioned role adaptation remains challenging, as demonstrations may contain uneven role distributions that limit generalization to underrepresented arm--role configurations. In addition, many bimanual policies predict actions in fixed left- and right-arm action spaces. While this provides a natural parameterization for robot control, it does not explicitly specify how behaviors should transform when functional roles are exchanged across arms. Across different scene initializations, the two arms may follow a similar coordination pattern, but the role-specific behavior assigned to each arm should change with the scene. Therefore, we propose BiRoAD, a Bimanual Role-Adaptive Decomposition framework for learning shared and role-adaptive representations in bimanual policies. Given bimanual trajectory or action-token features, BiRoAD decomposes these features into swap--symmetric and swap--antisymmetric components: the former captures coordination structure invariant to arm exchange, and the latter captures role-specific distinctions that vary consistently with functional role assignment. The two components are then recomposed as residual updates to the original paired arm representations, allowing BiRoAD to serve as a modular feature transformation without changing the policy inputs, imitation-learning objective, or requiring manually defined role labels. Across multiple bimanual manipulation tasks with balanced and imbalanced role distributions, BiRoAD improves robustness across role configurations over corresponding base policies, with notable gains on underrepresented role configurations.

</details>


### [83] [Smartphone GNSS Booster: Centimeter-Level Pedestrian Positioning Using a Portable Signal Re-Radiator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23456)
*Taro Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种外置GNSS信号再辐射系统，通过提升信号强度与抗多径能力，使智能机实现厘米级行人定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能机GNSS行人定位精度仅停留在米级，主因是紧凑型线极化天线导致观测噪声大、载波相位跟踪不稳定。

Method: 设计可直贴智能机的外置GNSS增强器，将紧凑有源螺旋天线与薄型无源贴片天线直连进行信号再辐射，仍复用智能机内置GNSS接收机与天线。

Result: 该增强器实现：提高接收信号强度以降低热噪声并稳定跟踪；相较线极化天线提升抗多径能力；稳定天线相位中心变化。静态实验表明载波相位观测质量显著优于常规智能机定位；行人实验中实现稳定的载波相位整周模糊度固定，达到厘米级定位。

Conclusion: 外置再辐射方案可在不改造内置硬件的前提下，将智能机行人定位精度提升至厘米级，为 sidewalk 级导航与精准位置服务提供可行途径。

Abstract: High-precision positioning using the global navigation satellite system (GNSS) embedded in smartphones is demanded for sidewalk-level pedestrian navigation and pinpoint location-based applications. However, current smartphone positioning accuracy for pedestrians remains at the meter level. This is mainly because limitations of the compact linearly polarized (LP) antenna in smartphones increase GNSS observation noise and hinder stable carrier phase tracking. In this paper, we propose an external GNSS signal re-radiation system that boosts smartphone GNSS observation quality while still using the smartphone's built-in GNSS receiver and antenna. The system directly connects a compact active helical antenna and a thin passive patch antenna for re-radiation, and is designed to be attached directly to the smartphone. The proposed GNSS booster achieves (1) reduced thermal noise and stable signal tracking via increased received signal strength, (2) improved multipath robustness compared with a LP antenna, and (3) stabilization of antenna phase center variation. Static experiments confirm a substantial improvement in the observation quality of GNSS carrier phase measurements compared with standard smartphone positioning. Furthermore, in pedestrian experiments, the proposed booster enabled stable carrier phase integer ambiguity resolutions, achieving centimeter-level positioning using a smartphone.

</details>


### [84] [STRIDER: Stepping-Enabled Multi-Gait Hierarchical 3D Loco-Manipulation Framework for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23483)
*Yuanzhuo Li,Wen Zhao,Zhe Yong,Xiang Meng,Gang Han,Hengle Ren,Xiaoyang Zheng,Zhen Wang,Yijie Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出STRIDER分层多步态框架，结合地形感知3D踏步逻辑、AMP自然行走与笛卡尔上身控制，并引入LD-PPO蒸馏算法，实现人形机器人精确落脚与全身操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形移动操作存在两大局限：连续速度指令控制器无法精确调节单个落脚点；专用落脚跟踪模块难以与全身操作集成。此外，标准基于动作的模仿蒸馏仅迁移专家动作，未显式促进异构技能共享表示。

Method: STRIDER框架集成地形感知3D踏步逻辑、基于AMP的自然行走和笛卡尔上身控制，其踏步专家在支撑脚坐标系中选择可行落脚点并生成考虑离地间隙的摆动轨迹。为将不同的行走与踏步专家融合为单一可执行学生策略，提出LD-PPO蒸馏算法，加入教师条件潜在对齐，联合优化在线强化学习、DAgger动作重建和潜在对齐。

Result: 在TianGong Omni人形机器人上的仿真与真机评估表明，LD-PPO在落脚跟踪和姿态跟踪精度上优于普通蒸馏PPO。部署到硬件后，STRIDER实现了具有精确落脚和末端执行器跟踪的多步态移动操作。

Conclusion: LD-PPO通过潜在对齐有效促进异构技能共享表示，使行走与踏步专家融合为统一学生策略，STRIDER实现精确落脚跟踪与全身操作的多步态移动操作。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation faces two prominent limitations: controllers using continuous velocity commands cannot precisely regulate individual footholds, while specialized foothold-tracking modules are difficult to integrate with whole-body manipulation. Furthermore, standard action-based imitation distillation primarily transfers expert actions, without explicitly encouraging a shared representation of heterogeneous skills. This paper introduces STRIDER, a hierarchical multi-gait framework to bridge these gaps. The framework integrates terrain-aware 3D stepping logic, Adversarial Motion Priors (AMP)-based natural walking, and Cartesian upper-body control: its stepping expert selects feasible footholds in the stance-foot frame and generates clearance-aware swing trajectories. To fuse distinct walking and stepping experts into one executable student policy, we propose Latent Distillation Proximal Policy Optimization (LD-PPO), a distillation algorithm augmented with teacher-conditioned latent alignment. By jointly optimizing on-policy reinforcement learning, DAgger-based action reconstruction, and latent alignment, LD-PPO transfers expert actions while encouraging a shared skill representation across heterogeneous modes. Simulation and real-robot evaluations on the TianGong Omni humanoid show that LD-PPO outperforms vanilla distillation-PPO in foothold-tracking and posture-tracking accuracy. Deployed on hardware, STRIDER realizes multi-gait loco-manipulation with accurate foothold and end-effector tracking.

</details>


### [85] [Cognitive Action Reasoning for Proactive Robots from Human-Centered Multimodal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23486)
*Zhihao Gu,Kechao Zhu,Yuanfeng Wu,Mohan Liu,Ankit Kumar Shaw,ChenDong Hong,Xuanyu Chen,Dengchen Mei,Xu Tianyi,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动机器人动作推理任务ProRobo，构建真实世界多模态数据集ProAction（10K样本，覆盖12种日常场景），并给出参考模型MMC2Act；实验表明通用MLLM在主动高层动作推理上表现不佳，而在ProAction上训练可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人在人类中心环境中通常执行显式指令，机器人学习数据集也多为观测与任务指令或低层动作配对。尽管已有主动式具身辅助研究，但现有资源面向不同设定和动作层级，真实世界人类中心的多模态决策仍缺乏探索。作者希望解决机器人在无显式动作指令时，依据多模态人类与环境线索主动决定动作这一上游认知决策问题。

Method: 作者将该问题形式化为Proactive Robot Action Reasoning (ProRobo)。为此构建ProAction：真实世界多模态数据集，含10K样本，涵盖视觉观测、音频信号与文本输入，覆盖5类常见场景中的12种日常场景。为获得具认知依据的高层动作监督，设计两阶段human-in-the-loop流水线：appraisal-guided候选生成 + Affective Theory-of-Mind引导的人工精修，在标注中显式引入对人类状态、紧迫性、可行性与潜在风险的上下文判断。基于此监督，对代表性MLLM进行基准测试，并提出参考模型MMC2Act，隐式学习从多模态观测到具认知依据高层动作的映射。

Result: 实验覆盖多种模态设置、主体不相交泛化、跨数据集迁移与人工评估：通用MLLM难以从多模态线索主动推理高层动作，而在ProAction上训练可显著提升性能。

Conclusion: 主动机器人动作推理是人机中心场景中的上游认知决策问题；ProAction提供了真实世界多模态监督资源，MMC2Act作为参考模型验证了在该监督下训练的有效性，说明通用MLLM在主动高层动作推理方面存在明显不足，需专门数据与训练方法支撑。

Abstract: Robots operating in human-centered environments are typically designed to execute explicit instructions, and most robot-learning datasets likewise pair observations with task instructions or low-level actions. Although recent work has begun to explore proactive embodied assistance, existing resources target different settings and action levels, leaving real-world human-centered multimodal decision-making underexplored. We formulate this problem as \textit{Proactive Robot Action Reasoning} (\textit{ProRobo}), an upstream cognitive decision problem in which a robot must determine which action to take based on multimodal human and environmental cues without explicit action instructions. To support ProRobo, we introduce \textit{ProAction}, a real-world multimodal dataset containing 10K samples of visual observations, audio signals, and text inputs across 12 daily-life scenarios in five common scenes. To construct cognitively grounded high-level action supervision, we develop a two-stage human-in-the-loop pipeline that combines appraisal-guided candidate generation with Affective Theory-of-Mind-guided human refinement, explicitly incorporating contextual judgment about human states, urgency, feasibility, and potential risk into action annotation. Based on this supervision, we benchmark representative Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and introduce \textit{MMC2Act}, a reference model that implicitly learns the mapping from multimodal observations to cognitively grounded high-level actions. Experiments across modality settings, subject-disjoint generalization, cross-dataset transfer, and human evaluation show that general-purpose MLLMs struggle with proactively reasoning high-level actions from multimodal cues, whereas training on \textit{ProAction} substantially improves performance.

</details>


### [86] [Design and Control of a Cable-Driven Switchable Actuator with Torque/Tension Dual Modes for Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23487)
*YuanLong Ji,Xu Liu,Xinyuan Cai,Qihan Ye,Xiangyu Xie,Ruizhe Jiang,Shuhan Xiang,Wenjing Liu,Qijun Wang,Yang Chen,Xingbang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可快速切换扭矩/张力双模式的绳驱动执行器（CDSA），将传感与驱动集中于近端，通过近端多源传感与数据融合估计远端力，实现双模式力控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴外骨骼机构通常仅具备单一机械输出模态（关节扭矩或沿肢体的线性牵引），难以适应多样化训练场景，且远端需布置传感器。

Method: 设计绳驱动可切换执行器（CDSA）：耦合动滑轮机构（CMPM）实现远端张力放大；双向绳驱动棘轮机构（CDRM）实现模式切换与预紧调节；全部传感与驱动集成于近端驱动单元，并以近端多源传感器配合数据驱动融合模型估计远端输出力；提出基于迭代学习控制（ILC）的自适应双模式力控策略。

Result: 平台实验测得扭矩模式传动效率(92.4±2.0)%、张力模式(96.5±3.3)%，张力放大比2.77±0.10；模拟膝关节步态轨迹与短行程张力曲线跟踪稳定，RMSE分别为未控峰值的(4.52±0.51)%与(3.15±0.19)%；坐姿人机耦合实验验证了两种模式下的可控力输出。

Conclusion: CDSA可在扭矩与张力两种模式间快速切换，并在无远端仪器条件下实现稳定、精确的双模式力控制，适用于关节扭矩与线性牵引两类训练场景。

Abstract: Existing wearable exoskeleton architectures are typically constrained by a single mechanical output modality, providing either joint torque around an anatomical joint or linear traction along a limb-training-oriented direction, which limits adaptability to diverse training scenarios. This letter presents a cable-driven switchable actuator (CDSA) that can rapidly switch between torque and tension modes while centralizing all sensing and actuation components at the proximal drive unit. A Coupled Movable Pulley Mechanism (CMPM) provides tension amplification at the distal end-effector, while a bidirectional Cable-Driven Ratchet Mechanism (CDRM) enables mode switching and preload regulation. To eliminate the need for distal instrumentation, multi-source proximal sensors are integrated with a data-driven fusion model to estimate distal output forces. An adaptive dual-mode force control strategy based on iterative learning control (ILC) is further developed. Platform experiments demonstrate transmission efficiencies of $(92.4 \pm 2.0)\%$ and $(96.5 \pm 3.3)\%$ in the torque and tension modes, respectively, along with a tension amplification ratio of $2.77 \pm 0.10$ under tension mode. Tracking tests on simulated knee-joint gait trajectories and short-stroke tension profiles yield stable control, with RMSEs of $(4.52 \pm 0.51)\%$ and $(3.15 \pm 0.19)\%$ of the uncontrolled peak value, respectively. Finally, seated human-coupled experiments validate the system's controllable force generation in both joint-torque and linear-traction application modes.

</details>


### [87] [FeasibleFlow: One-Step Joint Transport of Configuration Feasibility and Trajectories for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23488)
*Xiang Li,Bikun Wang,Qing Xu,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FeasibleFlow——一种单步端到端生成式框架，联合输运配置空间可行性场与多模态自车轨迹，并在NAVSIM基准上验证其性能与安全性机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶将当前观测直接映射到未来轨迹，但轨迹必须随场景演进而保持有效。未来状态建模可缓解这种时序错配，但通用表征常包含与自车规划无关的信息，且仅通过辅助监督、静态条件或候选评估影响轨迹生成，未能有效融入轨迹生成过程。

Method: 提出FeasibleFlow单步端到端生成框架，联合输运配置空间可行性场与多模态自车轨迹；设计Asymmetric Joint MeanFlow，利用MeanFlow恒等式中的路径雅可比-向量积将场的演化纳入轨迹输运；针对安全反馈比进度反馈更稀疏的问题，分别引入Anchor-relative ranker (ARR)用于候选选择、Pareto-ReinFlow用于生成，以平衡安全与进度。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准上的实验表明FeasibleFlow性能强劲，并验证了可行性与轨迹联合输运的有效性以及所提安全优先机制的作用。

Conclusion: FeasibleFlow通过联合输运可行性场与轨迹、并结合安全优先的候选选择与生成机制，为端到端自动驾驶中的时序有效性与安全-进度平衡提供了有效方案。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving maps current observations directly to future trajectories, yet those trajectories must remain valid as the scene evolves. Future state modeling aims to address this temporal mismatch, but general representations often contain information unrelated to ego planning and affect trajectory generation only through auxiliary supervision, static conditioning, or proposal evaluation. We propose FeasibleFlow, a one-step end-to-end generative framework that jointly transports a configuration-space feasibility field and multimodal ego trajectories. Our Asymmetric Joint MeanFlow uses the pathwise Jacobian-vector product in the MeanFlow identity to incorporate field evolution into trajectory transport. Because safety feedback is sparser than progress feedback, we further introduce the Anchor-relative ranker (ARR) and Pareto-ReinFlow to balance safety and progress in candidate selection and generation, respectively. Experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark demonstrate the strong performance of FeasibleFlow and validate both the joint transport of feasibility and trajectories and the proposed safety-first mechanisms.

</details>


### [88] [Elevator-VIGS: Separating Elevator Motion from Robot Motion in Visual-Inertial Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23491)
*Rui Zhou,Zihan Zhu,Wei Zhang,Zizhou Luo,Norbert Haala,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Elevator-VIGS 通过在电梯坐标系中估计机器人位姿、并将电梯相对世界的运动作为每关键帧的运输状态纳入稠密视觉惯性捆绑调整，实现了电梯行程中持续跟踪与建图，达到最优跟踪与渲染性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在运动的电梯内，相机只观测到机器人相对电梯的运动，而IMU感知的是该运动加上电梯相对世界的运动，二者冲突。现有视觉惯性估计器若以视觉为主导，只会跟踪机器人在电梯内的运动而忽略电梯上升；若冲突持续，则会导致估计器发散。同时现有方法难以在电梯行程中保持跟踪与建图。

Method: 作者指出冲突源于将两种观测强制统一到单一坐标系。Elevator-VIGS 改为在电梯坐标系中估计机器人位姿，并将电梯相对世界的运动建模为每关键帧的运输状态（电梯上升量与垂直速度），纳入稠密视觉惯性捆绑调整。系统使用视觉语言模型与深度网络零样本检测电梯行程，并在出发与到达时刻对运输状态施加约束。

Result: 在真实世界和仿真电梯序列上，Elevator-VIGS 取得了最优的跟踪与渲染性能；在四个无电梯的公开基准上，保持了 VIGS-SLAM 的最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦机器人相对电梯的位姿与电梯相对世界的运输状态，可有效解决电梯场景下的视觉惯性冲突，实现电梯行程中持续稳定的视觉惯性3D高斯泼溅SLAM，且不损害无电梯场景的性能。

Abstract: We present Elevator-VIGS, a visual-inertial 3D Gaussian Splatting SLAM system that keeps tracking and mapping through elevator rides. Inside a moving elevator, the two sensors are in conflict. The camera sees only the robot's motion relative to the elevator, while the IMU senses that motion plus the elevator's motion relative to the world. This conflict is challenging for existing visual-inertial estimators. If vision dominates, the estimator tracks only the robot's motion within the elevator and misses the elevator's rise, and if the conflict remains, the estimator diverges. We observe that the conflict comes from forcing both observations into a single coordinate frame. We instead estimate the robot's pose in the elevator's coordinate frame, and the elevator's motion relative to the world as a per-keyframe transport state, the elevator's rise and vertical velocity, within dense visual-inertial bundle adjustment. Elevator-VIGS detects rides zero-shot with a vision-language model and a depth network, and constrains the transport state at the departure and the arrival. We record real-world and simulated elevator sequences. On these sequences, Elevator-VIGS achieves state-of-the-art tracking and rendering performance. On four elevator-free public benchmarks it keeps the state-of-the-art performance of VIGS-SLAM. Project page: https://ruizhou-cn.github.io/elevator-vigs/.

</details>


### [89] [Imagine then Verify: Affordance-Targeted Active Perception for Task-Oriented Grasping in Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23504)
*Jingzhi Cui,Xuefeng Liu,Feng Han,Xinyu Liu,Wei Chen,Jianwei Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATAP：面向任务的抓取中，把主动视角规划从"完整扫描目标"转为"定向验证可供性"，用生成式形状先验想象被遮挡的目标几何以预测可供性分布，并以不确定性感知规划器联合优化熵减与可供性验证增益，比基于重建的主动感知减少57%以上NBV步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向抓取需抓取物体的功能性部位（如倒水时杯子的手柄），但这些可供性区域在杂乱场景中常被遮挡。现有下一最佳视角（NBV）方法只针对整个目标物体优化视角，不区分哪一部位与任务相关；若直接套用（先完整扫描目标再预测可供性），会把大部分视角预算浪费在任务无关表面（如倒水时的杯身）上。

Method: 提出ATAP（Affordance-Targeted Active Perception）框架，将视角规划由穷尽式目标扫描转变为定向可供性验证：1) 用生成式形状先验对被遮挡的目标几何进行假设补全，并在想象的完整表面上预测可供性分布；2) 针对杂乱场景严重遮挡导致隐藏可供性位置存在多解（歧义）的问题，设计不确定性感知的视角规划器，联合优化对竞争假设的期望熵减与来自真实观测的期望可供性验证增益；3) 迭代执行该过程直至可供性被充分验证，再执行抓取。

Result: 在仿真与真实杂乱场景实验中，ATAP相较固定视角的任务导向抓取基线显著提升了功能性抓取成功率，并优于基于重建的主动感知方法，NBV步数减少超过57%。

Conclusion: 以可供性为目标的主动感知（而非整体目标扫描或先重建后感知）能更高效地消除任务相关区域的遮挡，在少量视角预算下实现可靠的功能性抓取验证。

Abstract: Task-oriented grasping (TOG) requires robots to grasp functional parts of objects (e.g., the handle of a mug for pouring), yet these affordance regions are frequently occluded in cluttered scenes. Active perception via next-best-view (NBV) planning can resolve such occlusions by moving the camera for more informative observations. However, existing NBV methods typically optimize viewpoints for grasping the target object as a whole without distinguishing which part is task-relevant. A naive adaptation, fully scanning the target object before predicting the affordance, wastes most of the viewpoint budget on task-irrelevant surfaces (e.g., the mug body for pouring). To address this, we propose ATAP, an Affordance-Targeted Active Perception framework that shifts viewpoint planning from exhaustive target scanning to targeted affordance verification. ATAP hypothesizes the occluded target geometry via a generative shape prior and predicts the affordance distribution over the imagined complete surface. In cluttered scenes, severe occlusion can make the location of the hidden affordance ambiguous, leaving multiple locations plausible given the partial observation. ATAP therefore introduces an uncertainty-aware viewpoint planner that jointly optimizes expected entropy reduction over these competing hypotheses and expected affordance verification gain from real observations. This process iterates until the affordance is sufficiently verified for grasp execution. Experiments in simulation and real-world cluttered scenes show that ATAP substantially improves the functional grasp success rate over fixed-view TOG baselines, and outperforms reconstruction-based active perception with over 57% fewer NBV steps.

</details>


### [90] [PINGU: Extending Air-Bearing Spacecraft Emulators with Open-Source Actuators and Learned Control for Contact-Rich Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23554)
*Ricard Marsal I Castan,Akiyoshi Uchida,Aman Arora,Pedro Lima,Matteo El-Hariry,Anrej Orsula,Francesco Grella,Antoine Richard,Cedric Pradalier,Miguel A. Olivarez-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在开源ATMOS气浮平台上集成反作用轮与两条力/力矩感知机械臂（LEVION），通过统一ROS 2抽象层将经典最优控制与强化学习策略在同一硬件上无修改切换，构建PINGU系统并在四项基准任务中验证其接触富集与惯性耦合操控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本平面气浮台已成为自由飞行航天器GNC的标准替代验证平台，但现有平台基本仅配推力器，缺乏面向接触富集、惯性耦合操控的配置，难以覆盖接触动力学与多体耦合场景。

Method: 基于开源ATMOS测试台，加装一个反作用轮和两条带力/力矩传感的机械臂（LEVION），配可更换末端执行器；通过统一ROS 2抽象层将其作为一等控制执行机构集成。软件栈之上构建强化学习训练环境与数字孪生，并设计可同时承载经典最优控制器与学习策略的控制器，实现同硬件免修改切换。

Result: 在四项基准任务上验证PINGU：点位姿导航（经典LQR对比sim-to-real PPO）、机械臂引起质心偏移下的动态扰动抑制、反作用轮动量稳定、力控对接。结果表明新增执行机构将ATMOS级模拟器拓展至接触富集区间。

Conclusion: 该工作在单一可复现平台上打通经典最优控制与强化学习，拓展了ATMOS类气浮试验台的接触富集能力。

Abstract: Low-cost planar air-bearing testbeds have matured into a standard proxy for free-flying spacecraft GNC, but they remain largely thruster-only and are rarely equipped for contact-rich, inertia-coupled manipulation. Building on the open-source ATMOS testbed, we contribute a reaction wheel and two force/torque-sensed robotic arms (LEVION) with interchangeable end-effectors, integrated as first-class control actuators through a unified ROS 2 abstraction layer. On top of the software stack we build a reinforcement-learning training environment and digital twin, and a controller that exploits these added degrees of freedom, letting classical optimal controllers and learned policies be swapped on the same hardware without modification. We validate the integrated system, PINGU, across four benchmark tasks: point-to-pose navigation (classical LQR vs. sim-to-real PPO), dynamic disturbance rejection under arm-induced center-of-mass shifts, reaction-wheel momentum stabilization, and force-controlled docking. The results show that these additions extend an ATMOS-class emulator into the contact-rich regime and bridge classical optimal control and reinforcement learning on one reproducible platform.

</details>


### [91] [AR-WAM: A Visual-Conditioned Agent-Ready World Action Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23578)
*Yicheng Jiang,Zesen Gan,Xiaobo Wang,Tianlun He,Chenxu Zhao,Minghui Wu,Xinyue Wang,Jiaxu Wang,Junhao He,Jianan Wang,Qiming Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AR-WAM是一个视觉条件、面向智能体驱动的世界动作模型，用边界框和可学习操作token替代自然语言指令，仅0.5B参数，在多个基准上匹配或超越强基线并保持最低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型与世界动作模型依赖自然语言指令指定操作任务，该接口存在指代歧义、空间不精确、与智能体固有语言理解冗余、意图与执行纠缠等问题，不适合智能体驱动的机器人控制。

Method: 提出AR-WAM，采用两种互补条件替代语言：目标边界框的视觉定位提示（指定交互物体与位置）和可学习操作token（指定原子技能）。模型0.5B参数，视觉编码器冻结、无语言编码器，在紧凑潜在状态中预测场景演化并解码动作，通过显式可监督推理信号暴露策略意图；并提供模型无关兼容层（detect、execute、query三种原语）供本地VLM或在线智能体API直接驱动策略，长时程记忆与闭环错误恢复由智能体侧承担。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0、RMBench及真实Astribot S1双臂平台上，AR-WAM在标准操作任务上与最强基线持平（平均成功率87.2%），在依赖记忆与真实机器人长时程任务上分别提升5.9%和36.7%，同时保持最低推理延迟（14.1 ms）。

Conclusion: AR-WAM验证了以视觉定位提示与操作token替代语言指令的智能体就绪世界动作模型范式，在保持轻量与低延迟的同时提升了长时程与记忆相关任务表现。

Abstract: As AI agents become increasingly capable, agent-driven robotic control is emerging as a compelling paradigm. However, prevailing vision-language-action (VLA) models and world action models (WAMs) still rely on natural-language instructions to specify manipulation tasks, an ill-suited interface for agent-driven control: referentially ambiguous, spatially imprecise, redundant with the agent's inherent language understanding, and entangling intent with execution. We present AR-WAM, a visual-conditioned, agent-ready world action model that replaces language with two complementary conditions: a visual grounding prompt (a bounding box of the target) denoting the interaction object and location, and a learnable operation token dictating the atomic skill to execute. Our compact 0.5B-parameter model, with a frozen pretrained visual encoder and no language encoder, predicts scene evolution within compact latent states while decoding actions, exposing the policy's intent through explicit, supervisable reasoning signals. A model-agnostic compatibility layer provides three primitives (detect, execute, and query) so that local VLMs or online agent APIs can drive the policy directly, with long-horizon memory and closed-loop error recovery delegated to the agent side. On RoboTwin 2.0, RMBench, and a real Astribot S1 dual-arm platform, AR-WAM matches the strongest baselines on standard manipulation (87.2% average success) and outperforms them on memory-dependent and real-robot long-horizon tasks, improving success rates by 5.9% and 36.7%, respectively, while maintaining the lowest inference latency (14.1 ms).

</details>


### [92] [TaskAnchor: Grounding Task State in Reactive VLAs for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23580)
*Hengyan Liu,Wenlve Zhou,Bo Yue,Yongyi Su,Ruixiang Wang,Zhanqi Zhang,Dekun Lu,Wei Gao,Xiaofen Xing,Kui Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TaskAnchor，一种轻量适配器，通过将执行历史注入预训练VLA的视觉与语言接口来缓解"任务状态混叠"，在RMBench上平均成功率约为基线π0.5与X-VLA的4.9–5.5倍，每动作块仅增加2.08ms延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应式视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在长时程操作中表现不佳：视觉上相似的观测可能因任务阶段或交互历史不同而对应不同动作。作者将这一歧义定义为"任务状态混叠"（task-state aliasing），并试图在不引入显式规划器的前提下解决它。

Method: TaskAnchor由两部分组成：(1) 历史条件化的视觉精修（history-conditioned visual refinement）；(2) 里程碑监督的任务状态坐标（milestone-supervised task-state coordinate），即一个表征执行语义阶段的标量。两者分别通过原生视觉接口与语言接口注入，不修改动作生成机制。

Result: 在RMBench上，TaskAnchor平均成功率约为已发表π0.5与X-VLA基线的4.9–5.5倍；在RoboMemArena及真实机器人上取得一致增益；对π0.5每动作块仅增加2.08ms延迟。

Conclusion: 通过在执行历史层面为预训练VLA提供任务状态锚定，可在不增加规划模块、不改动动作生成机制且几乎不牺牲推理效率的条件下显著提升长时程操作性能，表明历史条件化与阶段标量是关键且低成本的改进方向。

Abstract: Reactive vision--language--action (VLA) models struggle with long-horizon manipulation when visually similar observations can correspond to different actions depending on the task stage or interaction history. We refer to this ambiguity as task-state aliasing and introduce TaskAnchor, a lightweight adapter that grounds pretrained VLAs in execution history. TaskAnchor combines history-conditioned visual refinement with a milestone-supervised task-state coordinate, a scalar representing the semantic stage of execution. These signals are injected through the native visual and language interfaces, respectively, without introducing an explicit planner or modifying the action-generation mechanism. On RMBench, TaskAnchor achieves approximately 4.9--5.5$\times$ the average success rates of the published $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA baselines, with consistent gains on RoboMemArena and real robots. The added latency is only 2.08\,ms per action chunk for $π_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [93] [PRIMO: Prior-Informed Odometry from Human-Motion Tracking for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23610)
*Xu Han,Angsong Li,Shaopeng Zhang,Enyu Li,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Yuan Zhuang,Haiyu Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRIMO 通过人类动作跟踪生成仿真监督数据，并结合物理与对称性先验的估计器，缓解人形机器人本体感知里程计的迁移难题，在真机实验中显著降低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真训练的人形机器人本体感知里程计存在两个迁移挑战：一是用于部署的特定控制策略生成的训练轨迹运动范围有限；二是仿真到现实的差距使无约束预测不可靠。

Method: 数据侧，让机器人在仿真中跟踪多样化的重定向人类动作以生成里程计监督，将监督与部署策略解耦并扩展训练动作分布；模型侧，提出 Prior-Informed 估计器，利用物理与对称性先验约束速度与旋转预测，并引入粗原始上下文通路，在保留编码特征的同时保留传感器上下文，以增强仿真到现实的泛化能力。

Result: 在统一真机协议下，PRIMO 相较各领域-指标比较中最强的外部基线，平均误差降低 31.6%-61.7%；跨两个运动策略版本，策略专家模型呈对称交叉现象，而 Tracking-Locomotion 训练使跨策略仿真平均误差降低 86.8%-94.6%；在真实动态动作上，相较在两个部署策略并集上训练，误差降低 69.2%-81.7%；在测试的动作组合中，Prior-Informed 估计器在仿真与真机中均持续降低平均轨迹误差。

Conclusion: 将监督数据生成与部署策略解耦、并引入先验约束的估计器，可有效提升人形机器人本体感知里程计的仿真到现实泛化与跨策略鲁棒性。

Abstract: Simulation-trained humanoid proprioceptive odometry faces two transfer challenges: training trajectories generated by specific robot control policies intended for deployment cover only a limited range of motions, while sim-to-real mismatch can make unconstrained predictions unreliable. We address both with Prior-Informed Odometry from Human-Motion Tracking (PRIMO). On the data side, we generate odometry supervision by having the humanoid track diverse retargeted human motions in simulation, decoupling supervision from the deployment policies and broadening the training motion distribution. On the model side, a Prior-Informed estimator uses physics- and symmetry-informed priors to structure velocity and rotation prediction and a coarse raw-context pathway to preserve sensor context alongside encoded features, thereby strengthening sim-to-real generalization. Under a unified real-robot protocol, PRIMO reduces mean error by 31.6%-61.7% relative to the strongest evaluated external baseline in each domain-metric comparison. Across two locomotion-policy revisions, policy specialists exhibit symmetric crossover, whereas Tracking-Locomotion training reduces mean opposite-policy simulation error by 86.8%-94.6%. On real dynamic motion, Tracking-Locomotion training reduces mean error by 69.2%-81.7% relative to training on the union of both deployment policies. Across the tested motion compositions, the Prior-Informed estimator consistently lowers mean trajectory errors relative to its Unconstrained counterpart in both simulation and real-robot evaluation. Code is available at https://github.com/Agibot-Spatial-Intelligence/PRIMO.

</details>


### [94] [CompVLA: A Variable Compliance Vision-Language-Action Model for Contact-rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23614)
*Jongmin Kim,Junsu Ha,Che-Sang Park,Minchang Song,Hyeokju Jeong,Himchan Hwang,Jianlong Fu,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CompVLA，一种从 RGB 与语言输入联合预测运动与刚度矩阵的统一 VLA 框架，通过专用 Compliance Expert 输出时变刚度与虚拟位移，并经几何阻抗控制执行，在接触密集任务上取得最高平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集操作不仅需要调节运动，还需调节机器人对外力的顺从方式，是 VLA 模型的前沿挑战。然而现有 VLA 仅输出纯运动学指令，缺乏力/柔顺性建模，导致真实世界接触密集任务性能下降。

Method: 提出 CompVLA 统一框架：在常规 VLA 架构上增加专用 Compliance Expert 模块，从 RGB 图像与语言指令中联合预测运动与刚度矩阵，输出时变刚度及虚拟位移轨迹，并通过几何阻抗控制执行。

Result: 在多种接触密集任务中取得最高平均成功率，优于 vanilla VLA 与具备柔顺感知的 VLA 基线；消融实验证实各组件均不可或缺。

Conclusion: 将柔顺（刚度）预测纳入 VLA 输出空间，是提升真实接触密集操作性能的有效路径，CompVLA 验证了运动与柔顺联合建模的必要性与优越性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation, requiring robots to regulate not only motion but also how they yield to external forces, has emerged as the next frontier for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. However, existing VLAs output purely kinematic commands, degrading performance on real-world contact-rich tasks. In this paper, we introduce CompVLA, a unified VLA framework that jointly predicts motion and stiffness matrix from RGB and language inputs. Our approach augments the conventional architecture with a dedicated Compliance Expert, which outputs time-varying stiffness and virtual displacement profiles executed via geometric impedance control. We demonstrate that CompVLA achieves the highest average success rate across diverse contact-rich tasks, outperforming both vanilla and compliance-aware VLA baselines, with ablations confirming each component is essential.

</details>


### [95] [Spiking Neural Network Actor-Critic Proximal Policy Optimization Control for Autonomous UAV Navigation Through Constrained Openings in Civil Infrastructure and Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23643)
*Francis Noah Walugembe,Maciej Wielgosz,Tomaž Goričan,Matej Mertik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的PPO算法用于约束三维环境下无人机自主导航，以降低深度强化学习的计算开销；采用脉冲actor-critic与随机高斯策略，实验完成1913/3000+回合，平均每回合通过2.10个窗口，成功率63.77%，后期阶段成功率超过90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束三维环境中无人机自主导航是机器人领域的难点，且广泛应用于桥梁、隧道与结构等民用基础设施巡检。深度强化学习虽在约束环境导航中表现良好，但其计算成本高，限制了在无人机自主导航中的实际部署，因此需要降低计算开销的算法。

Method: 提出基于脉冲神经网络（SNN）的Proximal Policy Optimization（PPO）算法，用于约束序列环境下的无人机自主导航。方法将基于脉冲（spike-based）的actor-critic强化学习与PPO相结合，并采用随机高斯策略进行导航决策。

Result: 在约束三维环境的无人机自主导航中，算法在3000+回合中成功完成1913个回合，平均每回合通过2.10个窗口，整体成功率为63.77%，并在算法后期阶段取得超过90%的成功率。

Conclusion: 基于脉冲神经网络的PPO算法可有效应用于约束三维环境下的无人机自主导航，在降低深度强化学习计算成本的同时实现较高且持续提升的成功率，验证了SNN与PPO结合在该任务中的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles in constrained three-dimensional environments has been a challenge in the robotics domain. The application of autonomous unmanned aerial vehicles in civil infrastructure inspection involves the use of such vehicles in bridge inspection, tunnel inspection, and structural inspection. The use of deep reinforcement learning in the autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles has been successful in constrained environments. However, the computational cost of the algorithm limits the application of the algorithm in the autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles. This paper proposes the use of the spiking neural network-based Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm in the autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles in constrained sequential environments. The proposed algorithm integrates the use of spike-based actor-critic reinforcement learning with the Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm. The proposed algorithm uses the stochastic Gaussian policy in the autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles. The proposed algorithm was implemented in the autonomous navigation of unmanned aerial vehicles in constrained 3D environments. The proposed algorithm was successful in completing 1913 episodes out of more than 3000. The proposed algorithm was successful in passing an average of 2.10 windows per episode. The proposed algorithm was successful in achieving a success rate of 63.77%. The proposed algorithm was successful in achieving success rates of more than 90% in the later stages of the algorithm.

</details>


### [96] [Beyond Appearance Shifts: Task-Semantic Action Calibration for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23650)
*Shuaijun Liu,Feiyang You,Chengyu Wu,Shuyang Hao,Chenglong Zhang,Jingyao Cai,Xingwei Chen,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BAS-VLA框架，在冻结的VLA基座上实现任务语义动作校准，兼顾行为稳定性与语义敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在具身操作中表现优异，但缺乏在行为稳定性与任务语义敏感性之间取得平衡的明确机制：在保持语义的外观变化下会产生多余的动作漂移；在语义发生破坏性变化时又无法产生足够不同的行为，仍沿用原轨迹。

Method: BAS-VLA构建于冻结的基座VLA之上，默认路径采用以"破坏"为中心的校准核心；并引入选择性的证据门控保持辅助模块，仅在检测到干扰性变化且任务语义保持一致时激活。

Result: 在OpenPI-pi0.5 / LIBERO-Object Milk-Swap基准上，干净条件成功率98.0%、语义保持条件97.5%；在刻意目标物体替换下，干净判据成功率降至0.0%，显示强的陈旧任务抑制与任务语义分离能力；在风格保持偏移上，成功率由42%提升至70%，且不损害干净性能。

Conclusion: 可靠的VLA行为不能仅依赖外观鲁棒性，而需要转向显式的任务语义动作校准。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance in embodied manipulation, but still lack a clear mechanism to balance behavioral stability with task-semantic sensitivity. We identify two complementary failure modes. Under task-preserving changes, where task semantics remain unchanged but scene appearance varies (e.g., style, illumination, clutter, or paraphrasing), policies often exhibit unnecessary action drift. Conversely, under semantic-breaking changes, where key task semantics such as the target object or constraint are altered, policies frequently fail to produce sufficiently distinct behaviors and instead follow the original trajectory. To address this gap, we propose BAS-VLA, a task-semantic action calibration framework built on top of a frozen base VLA. BAS-VLA adopts a breaking-centered calibration core as the default path, and introduces a selective evidence-gated preserving auxiliary that activates only when nuisance variation is detected while task semantics remain consistent. On the OpenPI-pi0.5 / LIBERO-Object Milk-Swap benchmark, BAS-VLA maintains high success on clean (98.0%) and semantics-preserving conditions (97.5%), while reducing clean-criterion success to 0.0% under deliberate target-object swaps, demonstrating strong stale-task suppression and task-semantic separation. On validated style-preserving shifts, it improves success from 42% to 70% without degrading clean performance. These results highlight that reliable VLA behavior requires moving beyond appearance robustness toward explicit task-semantic action calibration.

</details>


### [97] [WOLF: World Model Guided LiDAR Exploration with Predictive Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23656)
*Yuyang Tian,Penghui Yang,Pengyuan Wu,Haoran Yang,Chenhui Li,Pengfei Han,Dong Wang,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WOLF：一种世界模型引导的LiDAR无人机自主探索框架，通过预测未来观测生成"预测前沿"以增强探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LiDAR的无人机探索依赖已测量地图进行决策，难以预见遮挡后方空间的延续，导致有信息价值的方向被忽略，探索缺乏前瞻性。

Method: 训练阶段用循环世界模型从探索轨迹中学习观测动态，循环记忆保留跨视角空间上下文；推理时结合观测历史与候选运动预测局部占据与可见性；提出预测前沿生成机制，利用置信度、分支一致性与观测质量对齐融合预测，识别潜在高价值区域；预测前沿与实测前沿共同指导几何视点选择与轨迹生成，新扫描持续更新预测。

Result: 仿真中，Garage场景在相近覆盖率下平均终止时间较EPIC减少10.9%；Tunnel场景平均覆盖率由42.12%提升至98.35%；真实实验验证了学习模型在实体飞行中机载部署并在线推理的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过世界模型预测未来观测并生成预测前沿，可有效弥补遮挡导致的信息缺失，提升无人机探索的前瞻性与效率，并具备实机部署能力。

Abstract: LiDAR-based unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) exploration builds maps by continually selecting where to observe next. However, decisions based on the measured map provide limited foresight into spatial continuations behind occlusions, leaving potentially informative directions unrecognized. We present WOLF, a world-model-guided framework that predicts future observations to enhance autonomous exploration. In the training stage, a recurrent world model learns observation dynamics from exploration trajectories, with recurrent memory retaining the spatial context needed to interpret partial observations across successive views. Building on this context, the model combines observation history with candidate motions during exploration to predict local occupancy and visibility. To guide further sensing, a predictive frontier generation mechanism then aligns and fuses these predictions using confidence, branch agreement, and observation quality to identify promising regions. The resulting predictive frontiers join measured ones to guide geometric viewpoint selection and trajectory generation, while new scans update subsequent predictions. In simulations, our method reduces mean terminal time by 10.9% relative to EPIC in Garage at comparable coverage and increases mean coverage from 42.12% to 98.35% in Tunnel. Real-world experiments further demonstrate onboard deployment of the learned model for online inference during physical flight.

</details>


### [98] [UniPoint: Unified Point-Level Sensor Fusion for Humanoid Locomotion Across Challenging Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23666)
*Sicen Li,Zhen Chu,Chao Li,Qiuguo Zhu,Jun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 UniPoint，一个基于多源点级传感器融合的人形机器人全身运动框架，将 360° LiDAR 与两个深度相机早期融合为单一基座坐标系点集，通过体素化与线性自注意力编码实现与传感器数量无关的前向成本，单次训练即可在八种地形上部署，并在 DR02 人形机器人上完成多类真实场景验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界部署要求人形机器人安全穿越高度异构地形，感知需同时满足广覆盖、局部精度与传感器失效冗余三点。现有方案难以兼顾：单前向深度相机或近距高度采样覆盖不足；里程计校正的高程图在剧烈运动下漂移且漏检细薄垂直结构；图像级编码成本随相机数量增长。

Method: 采用多源点级传感器融合：将 360° LiDAR 与两个深度相机的测量早期融合为一个基座坐标系点集；经体素化重采样为固定数量的 token，由线性自注意力编码，并通过本体感知查询的交叉注意力处理，使前向计算成本与传感器数量解耦。点集可保留直立薄障碍；单模态失效仅移除部分 token，策略性能可优雅退化。单次训练引入地形感知奖励、感知退化注入与域随机化，产出一个适用于全部八种地形的统一策略，并直接部署于板载 RK3588，无需微调。

Result: 在 DR02 人形机器人上，于七种地形的九个真实场景中每个场景进行 20 次试验，验证了策略在 70 cm 高平台、100 cm 间隙、薄障碍以及稀疏或狭窄落脚点上的表现；同时可零样本泛化至户外环境。

Conclusion: UniPoint 通过多源点级融合实现了广覆盖、局部精确且对传感器失效具备冗余的感知，配合单次训练与板载部署，为异构地形下的人形机器人全身运动提供了一种统一且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Open-world deployment requires humanoid robots to cross highly heterogeneous terrain safely, with perception that simultaneously provides wide coverage, local accuracy, and redundancy against sensor failure. Existing approaches struggle to satisfy all three: one forward depth camera or nearby height sampling covers too little; odometry-corrected elevation maps drift under aggressive motion and miss thin vertical structures; image-level encoding costs grow with camera count. We present UniPoint, a humanoid whole-body locomotion framework built on multi-source point-level sensor fusion. Measurements from a 360° light detection and ranging (LiDAR) sensor and two depth cameras are early-fused into one base-frame point set. Voxelization resamples it to a fixed number of tokens encoded by linear self-attention and proprioception-queried cross-attention, decoupling forward cost from sensor count. The point set retains standing thin barriers; a single-modality failure removes only part of the tokens, so the policy degrades gracefully. A single training run with terrain-aware rewards, perception-degradation injection, and domain randomization produces one policy for all eight terrain types, deployed on an onboard RK3588 without fine-tuning. On a DR02 humanoid, 20 trials at each of nine real-world settings over seven terrain types validate the policy on 70-cm-high platforms, 100-cm gaps, thin barriers, and sparse or narrow footholds; it also generalizes zero-shot outdoors.

</details>


### [99] [Marginal Calibration Does Not Compose: Hidden Dependence in Modular Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23731)
*Rista Baral*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示机器人系统中各模块单独校准良好并不意味着组合后系统级不确定性仍保持校准，指出误差相关性是关键，并通过仿真验证联合协方差建模或依赖鲁棒界可改善下游规划与安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统由多个独立开发的模块（感知、预测、执行）组合而成。各模块单独测试时表现可靠并可能校准良好，但组合后系统级的不确定性校准未必保持。作者旨在研究模块组合对下游不确定性校准的影响，特别是误差相关性如何影响未来状态不确定性估计与规划决策。

Method: 采用移动障碍物预测流水线，使用位置估计器和速度估计器分别评估其单独校准表现，并比较在不同误差相关结构假设下对下游规划决策和安全性影响；在仿真中比较假设独立性、建模联合协方差、以及依赖鲁棒不确定性界三种方案。

Result: 位置与速度估计器在单独评估时都可显得校准良好，但其误差相关性的差异会导致未来状态不确定性估计显著不同。假定独立性会使系统过度自信或不必要地保守，直接影响下游规划决策与安全。建模联合协方差可恢复下游校准并提升系统性能；依赖鲁棒不确定性界则提升安全性但增加保守性（牺牲效率）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了独立验证机器人模块的根本局限：组件级校准不保证组合后的系统级校准。为此需要接口传递依赖信息，或支持直接的系统级校准，以确保下游规划的安全与有效。

Abstract: Robotic systems are typically composed of multiple independently developed modules that work together to perceive, predict, and act in the environment. Although each module may perform reliably in isolation, composing them does not necessarily preserve uncertainty calibration at the system level. In this work, we show that well-calibrated component interfaces do not necessarily produce calibrated downstream behavior after composition. Using a moving-obstacle prediction pipeline, we demonstrate that position and velocity estimators can each appear well calibrated individually, yet differences in how their error are correlated lead to substantially different estimates of future-state uncertainty. Consequently, assuming independence can make the system either overly confident or unnecessarily conservative, directly influencing downstream planning decisions and safety. Through simulations, we show that modeling the joint covariance restores downstream calibration and improves system performance, whereas dependence-robust uncertainty bounds enhance safety at the cost of increased conservatism. Our findings reveal a fundamental limitation of independently validating robotic modules and highlight the need for interfaces that communicate dependence information or support direct system-level calibration.

</details>


### [100] [FlockDiffusion: Assignment-Conditioned Diffusion for Multi-Drone Task Allocation and Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23745)
*Iana Zhura,Satenik Akopyan,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FlockDiffusion，一种结合场景图编码器、显式分配头、分配条件扩散Transformer和闭式轨迹解码器的学习框架，用于多无人机集群在复杂环境下高效任务捆绑与协同导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主多无人机导航需在紧凑计算预算下服务分散目标，任务捆绑（每架无人机沿途访问多目标）对高效协调至关重要。但将代价估计、分配与执行分置于独立求解器会导致冗余图搜索，并产生冗长、突变的路径。

Method: FlockDiffusion集成场景图编码器、显式分配头、分配条件扩散Transformer与闭式轨迹解码器；使用自回归教师模型提供离线监督，实现并行集群轨迹生成。

Result: PyBullet消融显示捆绑使任务完成率从50%提升至100%，完整教师相对带捆绑的MAGNNET降低路径代价8.4%。可扩展性基准中（每密度100场景、10架无人机），推理速度比经典流程快6.2–7.6倍，路径缩短约37%；任务数由20增至40时，延迟仅从7.8升至11.1 ms，基线为48.0至75.8 ms。Gazebo五环境评估中实现100%规划覆盖率，规划路径代价较带捆绑基线降低15.4%。

Conclusion: FlockDiffusion在多任务密度下实现高效规划，验证了端到端学习框架在难以用真实无人机集群复现的配置中的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-drone navigation requires fleets to service distributed objectives in cluttered environments under tight computational budgets. Efficient coordination depends on task bundling, where each drone visits multiple objectives along its route. Separate solvers for cost estimation, assignment, and execution incur redundant graph search and produce long, abrupt paths. We propose FlockDiffusion, a learned framework combining a scene graph encoder, an explicit allocation head, an assignment conditioned diffusion transformer, and a closed form trajectory decoder. An autoregressive teacher provides offline supervision for parallel fleet trajectory generation. PyBullet ablations show that bundling increases task completion from 50% to 100%, while our complete teacher further reduces route cost by 8.4% relative to MAGNNET with bundling. In the optimized scalability benchmark, evaluated on 100 scenes per density with ten drones, FlockDiffusion achieves 6.2 to 7.6 times faster inference and approximately 37% shorter routes than the classical pipeline. As nominal task counts increase from 20 to 40, latency rises from 7.8 to 11.1 ms, compared with 48.0 to 75.8 ms for the baseline. In a separate evaluation across five Gazebo environments, FlockDiffusion achieves 100% planner coverage and reduces planned route cost by 15.4% relative to the baseline with bundling. These results demonstrate efficient planning under increasing task density in configurations that are demanding to reproduce with physical drone fleets.

</details>


### [101] [EgoWild2Dex: Learning Dexterous Robotic Manipulation from In-the-Wild Human Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23755)
*Kunyang Lin,Xutao Wen,Jingxi Lin,Lanyong Lin,Jiaming Liu,Tianshuo Yang,Xianchi Chen,Yue Han,Yiduo Li,Zhanpeng Zhang,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出EgoWild2Dex，通过在真实环境中采集第一视角人类演示数据，结合几何变换器与人类-机器人联合训练，将野外人类经验迁移至双臂灵巧机器人，实现高成功率操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一视角人类数据多在受限或特制环境中采集，难以覆盖真实世界中长尾的对象与技能分布。论文旨在利用真实场景（家庭、工厂、药店等）中佩戴头戴相机采集的野外第一视角演示，为灵巧机器人操作学习提供监督，但此类数据存在场景杂乱和头部运动导致的视角剧烈变化（平均累积旋转15.93°/s）等视觉挑战。

Method: 论文提出EgoWild2Dex，通过分别对齐不稳定的第一视角图像与机器人观测、以及人类动作与机器人动作，将野外人类经验迁移至带灵巧手的双臂机器人。方法包含三部分：1）GeoFormer，一种可微几何变换器，将含噪的人类观测形变对齐至机器人观测；2）人-机器人训练方案，弥合具身差距，在有限机器人监督下实现高任务成功率；3）发布EgoWild数据集，含538.9小时野外第一视角人类数据、179,049个回合、125,961条唯一任务描述与1,282个对象类别。

Result: 在真实机器人上，EgoWild2Dex在三个长时程双臂灵巧操作任务上取得平均96.7%的成功率，对象级零样本平均成功率达33.3%。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了利用野外第一视角人类演示数据训练双臂灵巧机器人操作的可行性，通过几何对齐与跨具身训练方案有效应对视角不稳定和具身差异问题，并公开数据、模型与代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Egocentric human data provide a principled source of supervision for learning dexterous robot manipulation. Unlike prior approaches that often collect such data in constrained or specially constructed environments, we collect in-the-wild egocentric demonstrations in real-world settings, including homes, factories, and pharmacies, etc., where people perform their ordinary tasks while wearing head-mounted cameras. This collection protocol captures diverse workflows and hand-object interactions across long-tailed object and skill distributions, but also yields visually challenging observations due to scene clutter and head-motion-induced viewpoint changes (a mean cumulative rotation of $15.93^{\circ}$/s). To address these issues, we introduce EgoWild2Dex, which transfers in-the-wild ego-human experience to dual-arm robots with dexterous hands by jointly aligning unstable egocentric views and human motions with robot observations and actions, respectively. This work offers three benefits. First, we introduce GeoFormer, a differentiable geometric transformer that warps noisy human observations toward robot observations. Second, we design a human-robot training scheme to bridge the embodiment gap, enabling high task success with limited robot supervision. Third, we release EgoWild, a 538.9-hour in-the-wild egocentric human dataset comprising 179,049 episodes, 125,961 unique task descriptions, and 1,282 object categories. On real robots, EgoWild2Dex achieves an average success rate of 96.7% across three long-horizon bimanual dexterous manipulation tasks and an average object-level zero-shot success rate of 33.3%. The data, models, and code will be released.

</details>


### [102] [PackLab: A Comprehensive Framework for Developing, Training, and Evaluating MLLMs in Robotic Bin Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23784)
*Donghao Zhou,Jia-Hui Pan,Fan Zhang,Xingyuan Bu,Shilong Li,Xiaojie Gao,Yun-Hui Liu,Chi-Wing Fu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PackLab 框架，用于开发、训练与评估面向闭环机器人装箱的多模态大语言模型，包含仿真平台 PackLab-Suite、专用模型 PackLab-VLM 与基准测试 PackLab-Bench，实验表明其性能优于传统启发式、强化学习和通用 MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装箱是长时程序贯决策问题，每个物体的摆放会影响后续可用空间。现有方法主要依赖手工设计的几何启发式或针对固定训练配置的强化学习策略；尽管 MLLM 已有进展，但其在异构装箱配置下进行闭环序贯决策的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出 PackLab 综合框架，包含三部分：PackLab-Suite 提供基于物理的仿真平台，可扩展生成多样化装箱轨迹并评估物理结果；PackLab-VLM 是装箱专用 MLLM，理解动态变化的物体与容器状态，以闭环方式联合选择物体并预测放置位置；PackLab-Bench 提供多难度等级的标准化装箱场景用于系统评估。

Result: 大量实验表明，在不同物体集合与容器配置下，PackLab-VLM 平均优于传统装箱启发式方法、传统强化学习方法以及通用 MLLM。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了 MLLM 在长时程机器人装箱任务中的潜力。代码、模型、数据集与基准已开源。

Abstract: Robotic bin packing requires long-horizon sequential decision-making, as each object placement affects the available space for subsequent packing. Existing methods primarily rely on hand-crafted geometric heuristics that optimize predefined objectives or reinforcement learning policies learned through trial and error over predefined training configurations. Despite recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for this task, their potential for closed-loop sequential decisions across heterogeneous packing configurations remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce PackLab, a comprehensive framework for developing, training, and evaluating MLLMs for closed-loop robotic bin packing. PackLab-Suite provides a physics-based simulation platform for scalable generation of diverse training packing trajectories and evaluation of their physical outcomes. PackLab-VLM is a packing-specialized MLLM that understands the evolving object and container states to jointly select objects and predict placements in a closed-loop manner. PackLab-Bench provides standardized packing scenarios at multiple difficulty levels for systematic evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, on average, PackLab-VLM outperforms conventional packing heuristics, traditional reinforcement learning methods, and general-purpose MLLMs across object sets and container configurations, highlighting the potential of MLLMs for long-horizon robotic packing. The code, model, dataset, and benchmark are available at https://github.com/Correr-Zhou/PackLab .

</details>


### [103] [Risk-Aware Motion Planning and Control under Unknown Dynamics with Hybrid Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23792)
*Zhiquan Zhang,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对具有混合状态观测（盲区）的未知动力学机器人运动规划，作者在已有分层框架上，通过选取标称动力学并预计算开环控制序列来应对盲区，量化因真实动力学偏离标称模型而从未预期面离开盲多胞体的风险，将其嵌入随机迁移系统，并将高层规划建模为随机最短路问题以指导控制器综合，案例研究验证了方法在路径效率与盲区通行风险间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于系统辨识、预测可达性、图搜索与控制器综合的分层框架，依赖多胞形状态划分上的局部仿射近似模型，但要求在整个过程中都能获得状态观测以完成辨识与反馈控制，因而无法处理仅部分状态空间可观测（存在盲区）的场景。论文旨在弥补这一盲区下的规划与控制缺口。

Method: 在既有分层框架基础上，针对盲区采用标称动力学，并在观测丢失前预计算开环控制序列；由于真实动力学可能与所选标称模型不一致，机器人可能经由非预期面离开盲多胞体，作者对此转移风险进行量化，并将可能的输出结果纳入随机迁移系统；高层规划被形式化为随机最短路问题，以其策略指导底层控制器综合。

Result: 案例研究表明，该方法能够引导机器人从初始状态到达目标，并在路径效率与穿越盲区所伴随的风险之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 将盲区通行风险显式建模为随机迁移并求解随机最短路问题，可使分层规划框架在混合状态观测、未知动力学条件下依然有效，实现效率与风险兼顾的运动规划与控制。

Abstract: We consider robotic motion planning and control under unknown dynamics with hybrid state observations, where state measurements are available only in parts of the state space. Existing work combines system identification, predicted reachability, graph search and controller synthesis in a hierarchical framework using local affine approximated models over polytopic state space partitioning, but requires state observations for identification and feedback control. Based on this framework, we address blind regions by selecting nominal dynamics and precomputing open-loop control sequences before observation is lost. Since the true dynamics may differ from the selected nominal model, the robot may exit a blind polytope through an unintended facet. We quantify this transition risk and incorporate the possible outcomes into a stochastic transition system. The high-level planning problem is formulated as a stochastic shortest path problem, whose policy guides controller synthesis. A case study demonstrates that the method guides the robot from an initial state to a target while balancing route efficiency and the risks associated with traversing blind regions.

</details>


### [104] [ContactDP: Contact-Guided Diffusion Policy for Tight Insertion Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23800)
*Chengyi Xing,Shaoxiong Yao,Diego Romeres,Devesh K. Jha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContactDP：一种融合视觉、触觉与力觉的多模态扩散策略框架，配合混合位-力控制器，实现高精度连接器插入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人高精度连接器插入面临机械公差紧、接触过程部分可观测、以及由遮挡和接触歧义引起的多模态不确定性等挑战。成功插入依赖闭环接触引导，需持续融合全局对齐线索与局部接触反馈，在交互中生成稳定的修正动作。

Method: 提出 ContactDP（Contact-Guided Diffusion Policy），一种面向接触丰富插入任务的多模态扩散策略框架：联合融合腕部RGB观测、指尖触觉传感与腕装力-扭矩测量，用于推断接触状态并生成时间一致的修正运动；学习到的策略与混合位-力控制器协同工作，提供柔顺的低层交互。

Result: 在多种工业级连接器插入任务上（涵盖不同连接器几何、抓取条件和初始错位）进行评估，ContactDP 在性能、可靠性和泛化能力上均显著优于仅使用视觉的扩散策略。

Conclusion: 多模态接触反馈与扩散策略结合，并在混合位-力控制下执行，可有效提升紧配合插入任务的精度与鲁棒性，优于纯视觉方案。
